بسم الله الرحمن الرحيم

*Am back again👯🏻👯🏻*


*🅿 1*

Wata bakar mota kirar Bentley 2019 Mai bakin glass ce ta danno kanta cikin farfajiyar federal hospital dake cikin garin Kano, direct wata rumfa ta nufa wadda ajikin rumfar akayi symbol da babban rubutu aka saka *C. M. D parking lot* anan motar ta faka wani mutum daga gaba ya fito da sauri tare da bude murfin dama.

A hankali ya zuro da farar kafanshi dake sanye cikin wani black cover shoe na companyn Prada, kafin ya idda fitowa gaba dayan sa wooow na fada dan wani classic man ne ya fito sanye cikin wata dakakkiyar designer suit dark blue wadda tayi matukar amsar cikakkiyar halittar jikinsa, tabbas guy din ya hadu over dan kusan kamar shi ya ya zana kansa.

Daya murfin da driver ya zaga ya bude wata classic bab ce ta fito wadda kana ganinta kasan hutu da boko sun zaunar mata tana sanye da wata blue tokish gown ta yane kanta da bakin siririn veil kafarta da sanye da high heels baki mai shegen kyau,  tana da kyau amma ba sosai ba ba fara bace amma yanayin Hutu da jindadin da take ciki yasa fatarta ke wani irin glowing.

Wani sihirtaccen murmushi ya sakarwa drivern nasa har sae da siririyar hushiryarsa ta bayyana kafin ya furta.

"thank bukar. "

Matar kuwa sake kicin kicin tayi da fuska ta zagayo tare da shigewa jikinsa cikin wata murya mai dauke da kissa tace..

"baby let's go."

Ba tare da yace mata komai ba suka fara takawa cikin asibitin sakale da juna da kagansu kasan manyan masoyane, duk inda suka ra6a kallonsu ake tare da miko musu gaisuwa, amma shi kawai yake amsawa banda ita matar.

Har office dinsa na CMD ta rakashi sbd tsarinta ne kullum sae ta rakashi kafin ta tafi nata office din, kissing din bakinsa ta danyi kafin ta dago ta kalleshi da kyau tace.

"take care."

Gyada mata Kai kawai yayi kafin tasa Kai ta fice.



Dai dai bakin kofa ta bude kofar kenan sukayi gware da wata matashiyar nurse kyakkyawa number daya tana da shape mai kyan gaske domin boob's dinta a cike suke ga hip dinta nan ma tubarakallah irin matan nan ne da ake kira da full option.

Da sauri taja baya tare da rike files din hannunta da kyau tayi kasa da kanta jikinta na wani irin rawa lkc guda zufa ta rufets ruf tayi kasa da kanta muryarta na shaking tace.

"am..sorry...mah...ban...ban...san...ki....!"

cikin daka tsawa mai firgitarwa tace.

"shut up stub bitch! Are u nonsense?"

Dasauri ta girgiza Kai tana kuma fadin.

"sorry...doc...wllh... "

Mtsww taja wani dogon tsaki cikin isa da izza tace.

"get out of my side dabba."

Da sauri nurse din tabar wajen har tana hadawa da faduwa tabar wajen don tasan halin doctor fina kuma tasan wace ita.



*_MAHIR ABDULMU'IN ABDALLAH dane na uku wajen tsohon NGO na Africa wato Abdulmu'in Abdullah yan asalin kasar sudan ne mahaifiyarsa kuma yar Nigeria ce bafulatanar yola ce, mahir shi kadaine namiji a wajen iyayensa sai yarsa farida da kanwarsa najwa kuma dukkan nusu sunyi aure, family dinsu masu kudin gaskene kuma cikakkun yan bokone._*
*_duk da arziki na iyayensa da family basu baya daga cikin mutane masu dagawa can nuna arzikin da iyayensu ke dashi, yayi krtn shi na medicine a Oxford University anan ya zama kwararren likitan mata mai zaman kasa Mai tsananin basira da sanin aikinsa, yayi aiki da babban asibitin dake birnin London kuma asibiti na biyu a duniya gaba daya kuma yana daga cikin wadanda akeji dashi sosai a kasar indai ta sanin matsalar mace ne, ganin cigaban da yake samawa kasar da ba tashi ba kuma yana ganin halin da tamu kasar take ciki na rashin cigaba da kwararrun likitoci irinsu yasa ya tattaro ya dawo Nigeria domin suma su amfana da shi nan take yana dawowa aka bashi CMD na federal hospital ._*
*_doctor mahir yana kirki sosai ga tausayi da taimakon mararsa shi, kusan duk shekara yana kai mutane da dama saudiya aikin hajj, sannan yana da foundation guda ta tallafawa talakawa a garuruwan arewa dama kasar mahaifinsa Sudan._*
*_doctor mahir yana daga cikin the most eligible bachelor da muke dasu a duniya, wannan dalili yasa matarshi doctor fina ke matukar kishinsa dan da wuya mace ta daura idonta akansa taji batayi wani feelings nashi ba, gaba daya shekarunsa basufi 35yrs ba._*


*_doctor Nafisa Alkasim dan baiwa, 'yace d'aya tilo a wajen shugaban kasa mai ci a ynz wato alkasim dan baiwa, mahaifiyarta hajiya kuye kuma tsohuwar manager ce ta NNPC, asalinsu yan Nigeria ne nufawan Mina,  nafisa Fina tayi krtn ta na medicine a kasar Cyprus inda ta karanci abinda ya shafi hanci hunhu mak'ogwaro, tana da kwarewa sosai a aikinta Kuma tana daga cikin manyan likitocin da ake takama da su a Nigeria kmr mijinta._*
*_doc nafisa kwatata bata da mutunci bare da'a tana da jinkai da nuna isa da son asan ita ko wace, bata harka da kowa sai uwarta domin bata yarda da kowa ba sai dai tana da wani amintaccen yaronta guda daya wanda shine yasa kusan dukkan surrukanta,  tana da kishi Mai zafin gaske wanda ita kanta batasan wane iri bane domin zata iya aikata komai idn kishinta ya motsa, tana da gayu da kwalliya gata komai nata sak na yan bariki domin har tattoo ne da ita a wuyanta._*
*_tunda ta auri doctor mahir sbd tsananin soyayyar da take masa kasancewar mahaifinta aminin mahaifinsa ne, ba laifi shima yana sonta sae dea sam baya son halinta da dabi'unta, wajen aiki guda suke aiki sbd tsabagen kishinta duk wata staff mace daga patients har doctors nurses babu wanda bai san halinta ba, shi isa duk mai son kansa da arziki da zaman  lfy ba Mai shiga sabgar mijinta._*

Wannan kenan

Jikinta na tsananin rawa ta sauka daga benan CMD din ta nufi department dinsu na labour room, office dinsu ta shiga tare da ajiye files din hannunta ta zauna shirif tare da sauke wata nannauyar ajiyar zuciya. 

Daya daga cikin matan office din dake zaune tana rubutu a register ta ajiye beron hannunta tare da fadin.

"sister *HIBBATULLAH* lfyrki kuwa kika shigo haka kamar anjefoki ko biyoki akayi ne."

Wadda take gefe tana danna waya tace.

"kema dai sister rashida kamar bakisan daga inda take ba, wata kila tayi karo da madam."

"mtsw."

Kakeji wadda aka kira da Rashida taja tare da fadin.

"wllh kuna bani mamaki kun dauki tsoron duniya kun daurawa waccan ajawon mai siffar karuwai, ni banga abin tsoro a tare da ita ba."

Dasauri dayar ta tareta da fadin.

"aikuwa doctor Fina ita ke da abin tsoro, karfa ki manta ubanta ke da kasa."

Tsaki rashida taja kafin tace.

"aikin kutmar uba dan ubanta ke da kasa amma ai bashi ke da duniya ba, shikkenan dan tana matsayin yar shugaban kasa sae kuma akace ta taka kowa, wllh bari kiji ras nake da ita bana tsoronta ni wllh har so nake ma wani uziri ya kaini wajen CMD tym din kuma tana wajensa, tayi attempting yi mini wani Abu wllh sae na zubar mata da jini in yaso tasa a haka rijiya gaba dubu a jefani."

Hibba dake ta sauke ajiyar zuciya tayi bayyanan nan murnushi wanda ya fito da zahirin kyawun halittar da fuskarta ke dauke da shi sannan tace.

"um um dai sister Rashid banda cika baki."

"bar matsiyaciya sister hibba duk wannan zafin kan nata tasan doctor Fina taci uwarta iya shi."

"hmmm maman boy kenan ku zuba ido very soon rabb ya hadani da ita Zakusha mamakina."

"Allah ya hadaku kuwa."

cewar maman boy tana cigaba da abinda take, yayin da hibba ta Mike ta janyo dayar register ta fara cike cikenta...

*🅿 2*

Wani hamshak'in parlour ne da girmansa zeyi 100by100 fadin tsarin parlourn is weste of time kawai kuyi imagining kyawu da tsaruwar parlon shugaban kasa🤣😛.

Tana zaune sanye da skinny jeans da half vest ta daure gashin dokinta da akayi mata kananun kalba da shi tana danna wayarta kirar iPhone x-mas, karar taunar cingum dinta kawai ke tashi hade da tashin wakar dake fita a decorder.

A hankali yake saukowa daga stairs din parlon yana sanye da wasu designer english wears red T-shirt da black trouser ya zuba hannunsa daya cikin aljihu dayan kuma rike da car key dinsa da wayanshi. 

Har ya karaso cikin parlour hankalinsa na kan wayarshi da yake dannawa a gabanta ya tsaya tare da duban Rolex watch din dake tsintsiyar hannunshi yace. 

"baby ni zan dan fita."

Wani irin kallo tayi mishi mai dauke da ma'anoni iri iri sannan ta Mike tsaye tare da fadin.

"Bari nasako kayana muje na rakaka."

Sexy eyes nashi ya dan lumshe tare da waresu akanta yayi dan murmushi yace. 

"gidan Khalifa zani duba jikinsa I knw nasan bazaki ba."

Baki ta ta6e tare da mai da guri ta zauna tana kafeshi da ido alamar bata yarda dashi ba tace.

"ban yarda da kai ba."

"nata6a yi miki karya Fina."

"nop dear but... "

"don't saying me everything sai na dawo."
.
Ya karasa mgnr yana ficewa fuskan shi a hade.

Wata doguwar ajiyar zuciya ta saki kafin tayi saurin danna wayanta ta kara a kunne bugu biyu aka daga cike da bada umarni tace.

"gashi nan fitowa kabi bayanshi duk inda zashi."

Bata jira answer ba ta katse wayan tare da mikewa ta haye upstairs abinta. 

Dr Mahir ko da yafita direct shoprite ya nufa nan yaje ya judarwa yaran Khalifa sweet da chocolate d.s yarda yake tafiya so fine nd good ne yasa ya dauki hankalin duk matan wajen har ma da maza, sai dai ko tasu bayayi yana tsaye ya Mika ATM card dinsa wata matashiyar budurwa yar gayu tazo gabanshi tare da kashe murya tace.

"hello!"

kallo daya yayi mata tare da dauke kansa yace.

"hy"

"am dan Allah zan iya..."

Murmushi kawai yayi mata tare da daga mata Hannu yashige dominn tym din har an miko masa ATM dinsa.

Daskarewa tayi a tsaye da ganin kyawawan hakoransa da suka bayyana. Bayansa tabi da kallo tana imagining gata ta zama matarshi tana sakale a jikinsa sun zo shopping ajiyar zuciya ta saki tare da biyan kudin da tayi shopping itama ta fice.

Tana kokarin tada motarta akayi mata knocking zage galas din tare da kallon bakin basamudan dake tsaye akanta wani yawu ta hadiye da kyar tare da fadin.

"malam lafiya."

A hankali ya zuro da hannunsa cikin motar tare da kai mata wani kyakkyawan naushi a kumatu har sae da ta saki kara hawaye na zubota ta dago ta kalleshi a fusace zatayi mgn yayi saurin katseta cikin harshen turanci yace. 

"meye hadinki da gayen can."

Wani kallon rainin sense tayi masa kafin ta mAyar masa da cewa.

"ban sa..."

Maganarce ta rike lokacin da idonta ya sauka kan bindigar dake cikinsa jikinta take ya fara tsuma ta fara kuka wiwi tana rokanshi. 

"wllh billahil azim ban sanshi ba,wllh bai ma kulani ba dan girman Allah kayi... "

"shiii, ki kiyaye."

Daga haka yabar wajen, tana ganin ya shiga motarsa ta fito tare da saka gudu.... 




Isar sa gida Khalifa dake unguwar jambulo bayan yayi parking a harabar gidan fita yayi tare da karasawa gaban mai gadin gidan fuska a sake ya mika masa hannu tare da dan rissinawa yace. 

"barka da yamma Baba. "

Baki Mai gadin ya bude ganin girman da ya bashi sannan ya amsa cikin sakin fuska. 

"barka dai dan albarka, alhj Kake nema."

"eh baba."

"bari na yi ma sallama dashi"

Daga haka ya isa cikin gidan,  fitowa yayi tare da sanar masa cewa ya shigo, ledojin cikin motar ya fito dashi tare da basa ya shiga shi.


Yana isa cikin parlourn da ya hadu sosai shima, Khalifa ne zaune Wanda kusan shima a shekaru zeyi mahir din fuska a matukar sake ya Mike ya nufi mahir din yana fadin.

"your welcome Dr."

sukayi hugging juna cike da tsantsar farinciki kafin su zauna suna sake gaisawa Khalifa yace.

"Dan iska Ashe Kai ne shine har da wani turowa neman iso kamar gidan bakonka ne."

"Addini ne ya koyar damu haka ai, oh naga gidan shiru ina mutanen gidan."

"sun dauka bako ne ae, bari na kirata."

Daga inda yake zaune ya kwalla kira da fadin.

"Ramla!!!"

"na'am."

"kizo mahir ne fa."

ai kafin kace mene sae ga yara nan guda biyu mace da namiji Sun fito da gudu suna tsalle suna ga daddy ga daddy. 

Hada yaran yayi gaba daya yayi hugging cike da so da kwanar yaran, suka dago tare da tsugunnawa suka ce.

"good afternoon daddy."

"afternoon sweethearts, how ur study? "

"alhmdllh daddy."

Murmushi yayi tare da shafo kansu, ramla dake zaune gefan mijinta sanye da dogon hijjab fuskanta dauke da fara'a tace.

"yau doctor ne a gidan namu kenan."

"Mrs Khalifa how far ya jikin katon mijinki."

Gaba daya dariya sukayi kafin Khalifa yace. 

"kaji dan iska nine katon?"

"gashi nan kuwa kana zaune katonka da kai ka nade wai sunan baka da lfy."

dariya suka sakeyi dai dai shigowarta sanye da riga da skirt na yan kanti ta yane kanta da katon mayafi, hannunta dauke da katon tire wanda ke shake da kayan sha dana ci, cike da nutsuwa ta ajiye kan center table din dake tsakiyar parlon ta ajiye tare da janyoshi gabansa ta ajiye, kanta a kasa cikin siririyar muryarta mai taushi tace.

"ina yini."

Sai lokacin ya dauke idanuwansa daga kanta domin sosai nutsuwarta ta tafi da imanin shi cikin sanyi shima ya amsa da fadin 

"lafiya lao, yh gida."

"alhmdllh."

Ta fada tana mikewa zata wuce,  unty ramla tace.

"hibba ki zuba masa lemon mana."

"no tabarshi idan zansha na zuba thanks."

Wucewa tayi ba tare da tace komai ba, wata 6oyayyiyar ajiyar zuciya ya saki tare da maida idonsa kanta har ta shige.

Hira sosai suka zuba har magriba yana gidan kafin yayi musu sallama ya tafi. 






Washe gari 

Tun safe hibba ta tashi kamar yarda ta Saba, tashiga kitchen ta fara hada masu breakfast Mai aiki na taimakonta, 7:00 ta kammala komai nata ta gyara ko ina tsaf, dakin yaran gidan ta shiga tare da fara tashinsu. 

Wanka tayi musu cikin sauri tare da shiryasu tsaf, cikin uniform dinsu parlour suka fito dai dai fitowar ramla da Khalifa cikin shirinsu na fita sai yar yarinyar dake hannun ramla Khalifa yace. 

"unty hibba sarkin hidima  Allah dai ya saka da alkhairi."

"amin, aini wllh bansan ya zanyi ba idan hibba tayi aure ba Dan gaba daya ta gama sangartani."

murmushi hibba tayi tare da amsar Amna tace.

"kawota kuyi breakfast karku makara Bari naje nima na shirya."

"na dauka you evening ne  dake ai."

Unty ramla ta fada tana zama kan kujerar dinning

"ae ba'a kafe ruster ba tukunna."

"OK."





*_HIBBATULLAH muktar ammani shine sunanta, yan asalin garin Kano ne cikin karamar hukumar sumaila Mamata kuma yar gombe ce, su bakwai ne a gidansu maza biyar mata biyu, hibba itace ta shida a gidansu sai kaninta, yayin da ramla ita Hibba take bi._*
*_babu laifi suna da rufin asirinsu mai kyau, domin kaf yanyan gidan suna da iliminsu na addini dana Boko, hibba yarinyace mai hakuri da juriya tare da kawaici, tayi makarantar school of nursing har ta fara aikinta a federal hospital, shekarunta 26 hakan yasa iyayenta suka takurata sosai akan aure amma Allah bai kawoba wannan dalili yasa ta dawo hannun yayarta da zama._*

Wannan kenan.

*🅿 3*

Wanka tayi ta fito tare da shafe jikinta da lotion dinta mai dadin kamshi da gyara fata tare da saka fata santsi da kyau na hutu na musamman sunan Man doctor clear, powder kawai ta shafa sai lipstick ta sanya fararen uniform dinta wanda suke a goge tas, doguwar abaya baka ta daura a saman kayan kafi tasa dan hijjab dinta fari da ya sauko mata har kasan kafadarta,  wani hadadden brown din glass mai shape din cat eyes ta dauka ta saka, yayi cif da tsarin cikakkiyar fuskarta yayi mata kyau sosai, body spray ta dan fesa kafin ta dauki Jakarta da takalminta ta fito rike da Amna a hannunta. 

Ba kowa a parlon sai mai aiki dake tattare kayan da akayi breakfast dasu.

A kan kujera ta ajiye Amna sannan tashiga kitchen breakfast nata ta juye a food flacks sbd ta makara tare da sawa a jaka tafito tana duban diamond watch din dake hannunta tace.

"Kamila ga Amna kiyi mata wanka plz ki sake gyara gidan, untyn ta fada miki abinda zaki dafa da Rana? "

"ah ah tace dai idan kin fito na tambayeki abinda za'a dafa din."

"okay kiyi mana rice and beans, ki daka mana yaji mai dadi kisa tafarnu cikin yajin, ungu kibawa iliya mai guga ya siyo salad, idan nadawo nayi na dare. "

Ta fada tana mika mata 100 naira.

"toh sai kundawo Allah ya kiyaye. "

"amin, ki dauki Amna kuyi baya da ita kartaga fitana tayi kuka."

Dariya Kamila tayi tare da daukar Amna tana mata wasa suka shige kitchen ita kuma ta fita.

Sai da suka gaisa da mai gadi kafin ta fito, da yake gidan nasu a bakin titi yake tsaye tayi sai ga Mai napep nan ta shiga suka kama hanya.


Yau ma kamar kullum Mr and Mrs Dr Mahir tare suka zo yau ma the same color kaya suka sa, suka jero kmr ka sacesu sbd tsanin kyau, yana shiga office nasa ya bada sanarwa cewa yana son meeting da duk wata nursing dake labour room by 10:am.


Sai 9 ta iso cikin asibitin,  office nasu ta shiga tayi sign kafin ta ajiye Jakarta tana fadin.

"sannun ku da aiki sisters mun tashi lafiya."

Wata ta dan ta6e baki sannan tace.

"Ke dai wllh babu abinda yake duminki sister hibba kullum kara bunkushewa kike kina wani kara kyau meye sirrin ne."

Shewa mutanen dakin suka saka gaba daya maman boy tace.

"in banda abinki sister khady itafa hibba mai kyauce tun usuli kuma mene ze dameta da ranta da lfyrta da yan chanjinta kinga kuwa ae zero damuwa."

Wata mata da shigowarta kenan tana cire hand suck daga gani daga aiki ta fito tace.

"hibba itace ke da damuwa kuwa."

Ido hibba ta zaro tare da fadin.

"wata damuwa nake da ita ni 'yasu mum ashraf."

"damuwar rashin aure mana, shekaru 26 ace mace babu namiji kusa da ita ai kuwa wannan macen ita ke da zundumemiyar damuwa kuwa."

Rashida da tun dazu take ta rubuce rubucenta bata tankasu ba tace.

"hmm aikin kawai yo ita har shekarun nata nawa suke a gurin nan fa har da yan shekara 35 sai ita yar 26 kuma komai ai da lkcn sa ko zata dauki kanta ta Kai gdn mijin ne, kuma da wani gidan mijin ba gwanda gidan uba sai dubu ba, su masu mazajen har Mai zasu nuna mana,  basai dai suce su ana cinsu ba."

"ke sister rashida karkuma fa ki fadi mgn anan."

Cewar maman ashraf cikin daga murya. 

"na fada zaki dakeni ne, ko kinji na fadi sunanki ne kukan kurciya nayi kuma na baki amsa dai dai da tmbyr ki."

"to dake aike ji nake sai kibari a kasa sannan ki dauka."

Cewar khady tana daka mata harara. 

"ba'a kasa daniba amma na dauka sbd kunga ita bata da baki shikkenan ko wacce Shara kuka dauko kwa zuba mata, ni zanzo watarana wata yar bura uban nan ta fadan mgn kwatan kwacin yarda kuke fadawa hibba yarinya taga yarda ake gurza baki aikin bnz baku da aiki sai gulma da munafunci da sa idon bala'i alhalin babu abnd bamu sani ba mtsw"

Fada ne ya kaure tsakanin su uku maman ashraf rashida da khady sai fadawa junsu mgn suke, shigowar metro yasa sukayi shiru cikin hada rai tace.

"jarababbu yan kata'i kullum ku kenan cikin masifa,  babu yarda za'ayi aji department din nan shiru kuna nan kuna jidalinku kumbar parties kwance sbd babu naku aciki ko? Yayi kyau kuma kuyi da hujja dan CMD duk yana da wani record na rashin mutuncin da kuke da shegen rashin kula da patiies babu abnd HOD bai sanar dashi ba, sbd hka kuje yana neman ku."

Gaban hibba ya cigaba da dukan uku uku suka Mike suka fita har masu aikin yamma da na kwana da masu hutu duk sunzo, hibba suna tafe ita da rashida hibba ta dubi rashida da har ynz mita takeyi tayi dan murmushi tace.

"plz sstr rashida adinga hkr da juna a dinga Kai zuciya nesa."

Harara ta 6alla mata tace.

"ke ai daman baki san ciwon kanki ba idan ke zakiyi hrk wllh ni bazanyi ba dan banga yar kutmar uban da zatayi mini in kyaleta ba ko wace wllh, shegu duk bamuna da tarihinsu a gdn mazajen nasu ba, ita maman ashraf din bakar munafuka da komai aka gani ajikinta tace abban ashraf ne yayi mata to wllh duk karya take ko farcen susa bayayi mata komai da kanta takeyi hatta da kudin mkrntr yara ita ke biya kuma a haka ya karo mata kishiya kudin hayar gdn da suke zaune ita ke biya, ita khady dan uwarta taji ta tension din dan iskan mijinta manemin mata dan giya har kan gadonta yake kawo karuwa, yaje kuma yayi tatil dinsa yazo aci uwarta yayi mata a mai ta goge kuma, to idan kuwa irin aurensu zakayi ba gwanda ka dawwama gidan ubanka ba."

"hmmmn sstr rashida baki da dama ke dae Allah ya kyauta kawai zamuce."

Sae masifa take har suka karasa black din CMD din, a babban dakin taro dake office dinsa suka taru mazansu da matansu,  dakin ya gama hAduwa da tsaruwa sai kamshi yake, kowa an ajiye masa lemo da ruwa, zancen su ke tashi kawai kafin kamshinsa ya kuma gauraye gurin ahnkl ya fito ta wata kofa hannunsa rike laptop dinsa ta companyn apple, sanye yake da wata arniyar amecian suit dark ash yasaka red T-shirt da red cover shoe hatta da apple watch nashi red ne fuskar nan ta kara kyau da kyalli kyakkyawar kasumbarshi Mai yalwa ta sake wani baki ga gashin kansa dake kyalli anyi masa style mai tozo daga sama abin burgewa, fuskarsa dauke da fresh smile ya Zauna tare da sallama.

Daga mazan har matan babu Wanda be tafi da a tension dinsa ba har masu aure, hibba da tayi niyyar yi masa kallo daya sai gata ta tsinci kanta da kasa dai na kallonsa. 

cikin sahihin turanci Wanda kanaji kasan meyi yana da kyakkyawar alaka da turawa domin turancin da yake ko wani baturan albarka, jawabi yayi sosai tare da jan hankalinsu akan muhimmacin aikinsu na kar6ar haihuwa sannan ya gargadesu sosai akan Mai da hnkl da zuwa da wuri sannan da kula da mararsa lfy sosai yarda ya kamata, daga nan ya bada dama idan da akwai mai shawara ko wani korafi yayi mgn. 

Kowa kasa mgn yayi sbd tsabagen kwarjini da haiba ta Dr Mahir, cikin nutsuwa da karfin gwiwa duk da gaban na tsananta bugu ta Mike, kallo gaba daya ya koma kanta dago da kansa yayi ya kalleta da kyau idanuwansa kan hip dinta dake bayyane sosai  cikin wandonta, Allah yayi shi mutun Mai tsananin son mace hip, gyara zaman glass nata tayi da kyau kafin ta fara mgn cikin zazzakar muryarta. 

Korafe korafen rashin wadatattun kayan amsar haihuwa ta fara bayarta tare da rokon a gyara masu office dinsu tare da bukatar gadaje da while chair mararsa lfy, nan fa kowa ya fara kuskus domin kusan abnd ke ran kowa, nan ta Kara da cewa ajawa doctors nasu kunne domin wata haihuwar tafi karfinsu dole sai da likita amma basa samun goyan baya, nan ma tafi aka sa daga karshe tayi addu'a da godia ga CMD cikin nutsuwa ta zauna gaba daya aka saka mata tafi don tayi mgnr dake zuciyar kowa, sosai ta burgeshi da haka aka tashi daga taron shi ya fara fita tare da radawa PRO dinsa Wata mgn a kunne ya shige.

Ita da rashida ne karshen fita har sunje bakin kofa, pro yace, 

"hello maah."

"hy"

"doctor yace ki same shi a office now. "

Kallon rashida tayi itama rashida ita din take kallo cikin rawar murya tace.

"nashiga uku rashida me nayi."

Dariya rashida ta fashe da ita tare da dan bugun kafadarta tace.

"see you, ke me isa kika fiye tsoron tsiya ga yarda kika wani hargitse Kamar wadda ze Kama."

"amma sstr kinsan halin matar shifa kartazo ta...."

"mtsww dillah forget wannan stubborn din kije kiran oganki."

jiki a sanyaye suka fita tare da nufar office din marairaice face tayi sannan tace.

"Dan Allah sstr ki rakani."

"wa wannan fadan ba nawa bane naku ne na Manya."

Ta fada tana dariya, harara ta 6alla mata tare da tura kofar a hnkl cike da nutsuwa.......

*🅿 4*

A hankali ta murd'a k'ofar ta shiga kanta a k'asa, tare da furta sallama cikin sanyin murya. 

Yana rataye coat dinsa ya amsa mata ba tare da ya kalleta ba har ya zauna, kansa akan wayarsa da ake kira ya daga tare da zama yana kafeta da ido ganin yarda jikinta ke Dan rawa daga gani kasan a takure take da kallon da yake mata.

Kwas kwas kake jin karar takalmi tare da sautin muryarta cikin fada tana bala'i, gaban hibba ya yanke ya fadi jikinta ya hau rawa idanuwanta suka fara ciccikowa da hawaye tare da waresu kan kofar shigowa, shi kuwa ko ajikinsa relaxing yayi kan kujerarsa yana cigaba da wayarsa da mum dinsa cikin kwanciyar hankali, bata gama shiga tashin hankali ba sai da tajita daf kofar tana kokarin shigo, da gudu tayi wani tsalle sai gata bayan Dr mahir hawayenta na kuma kwaranya wane an bude famfo,cikin muryar kuka ta zuro kanta kan shoulder dinsa tare da fadin.

"Dr plz help me ur wife..."

Mgnr ce ta rike lkcn da ta bude kofar still kanajin muryarta tana zazzaga bala'i sai da ta tsaya chak ba tare da ta shigo ba.

Da karfi ta rintse idanuwanta wani fitsarin wahala na cika mata Mara, gaba daya baya tunaninsa baya jikinsa coz yarda tayi masa mgnr sosai ya haifar Masa da wata irin kasala. 

Kokarin tattaro nutsuwarsa yayi tare da nuna mata wata kofa dake gefe da gudu ta mike ta shige dai dai shigowar madam...... 



*******

"dole ne hibbatullah ta dawo gidan nan kuma ta fito da mijin aure domin nagaji Da surutun mutanen unguwa ace mu zubawa yarinya idanu sai abinda taga dama zatayi,  to kinji abinda na fada miki dai ki kiraro ramlatun lallai lallai yau ko gobe ta dawo gidan nan lada ya isa haka."

Daga haka ya gyara babbar rigarsa ya fice, umma dake tsaye tayi ajiyar zuciya sannan tabi bayansa da kallo, tafi 10 minutes tsaye gurin kafin taja kafa ta shige cikin bedroom dinta.




*****

Wani kallo take bin kofar da hibba ta shiga kafin ta Kalli mahir da ko inda take be kallaba idanuwansa lumshe, kofar ta nufa da niyyar shiga cikin isa mahir yace. 

"stop! "

Ko ta kansa batabi ba ta cigaba da takawa gabanta na wani faduwar da tunda take bata ta6a jin irinta ba.

Cikin karaji da daka tsawa ya kuma cewa. 

"stop mana!!! "

Juyowa tayi a hargitse tare dayi masa wani irin kallo mai dauke da ma'anoni iri iri cikin nuna isa tace.

"me kake 6oyewa."

"kina tunanin akwai abinda zan 6oyene."

"tunda muke da kai ka hanani shiga...."

Maganarce ta tsaya chak lkc kiran waya ya shigo wayarta, kallonsa tayi a shekeke tare da yin kwafa tafice tana receiving call din.

Kusan lkc daya suka sauke ajiyar zuciya shida hibba, da sauri yazo yasawa kofar key sannan ya shiga dakin da hibba ta shiga, tana tsaye tsakiyar dakin duk da sanyin dakin amma ita gaba daya ta gama jikewa kamar wadda tayi wanka da ruwa, idanuwan nan nata kamar su fado sbd firgici,  tsaye yayi tare da jingina jikin door din ya harde hannayensa a kirjinsa, yana kallonta, shi dariya ma ta bashi yayi dan murmushi sannan yace.

"na dauka ke jaruma ce."

shiru tayi batare da tace masa komai ba, a hnkl yake takowa ganin yana nufosa yasa ta faraja da baya tare da sake fiffito da idanu, duk da haka tun karota yake cikin rashin sa'a tayi tuntu6e da jikin flat bed en dakin, luuu tayi zata fadi yayi saurin tallafo bayanta ta fado jikinsa, har kirjinsa da nata na haduwa, wani mugun electric shock sukaji musamman ita da wannan ne karonta na farko da hada jiki da wani namijin da ba muharraminta ba.
Kallon junansu suke eyeballs to eyeballs ko wanne bugun zuciyarsa na dadduwa, ita ta fara janye jikinta ganin tana kokarin rasa control dinta .

Waskewa yayi tare da fadin.

"ki dinga kula."

"tohm. "

Tace a hnkl. 

"ta fita ita dodon naki zaki iya fitowa ki tafi and... "

Sai kuma yayi shiru yana ficewa daga dakin.

Wata ajiyar zuciyar ta saki sannan ta nufi dressing mirror ta tsuwa kanta ido na yan sakanni ganin duk yarda ta hargitse murmushi ta danyi kafin ta furta.

"matarce bata da M yau da ta kamani tab."

Ta fada tana dariya, handkerchief ta ciro a Jakarta tare da goge fuskarta, ta dan gyara hijjab dinta tare da mayar da glass dinta, ta dubi fuskarta sosai taga tayi kafin ta fito a hnkl.

Bai dago daga aikin da yake a laptop dinsa va taji yace. 

"kingama kwalliyar."

Ido ta dan zaro tare da rike baki tace.

"kwalliya kuma, ni ba kwalliya nayi ba."

shiru yayi mata Kafin tace.

"can I go?"

"yes."

Ya fada a takaice, dan rissinawa tayi tace thank u sir. 

Daga hka ta kama hanya zata fita yabi bayanta da kallo ganin yarda tana tafiya hips nata yana motsawa yasan kuma maza da dama sun ganta a haka a gadarance yace.

"ki kara girman hijjab din jikinki yayi kadan ko dan Kare darajar kanki."

Daga hka ya Mike yabar wajen ransa a 6ace....

Ido tabishi da shi kafin tace.

"oh ni yasu ko ina ruwansa oho"

Nima dea nace oho kuma kuce oho🤣🤣🤣🤣

*🅿 5*

Department dinsu ta koma zuciyarta cike da tarin tambayoyi game da Dr mahir gashi dai turawa yayi ayi kiranta amma ita bataga abinda yace mata ba sai ma kusan janyo mata da yayi ta rasa aikinta ga wani irin mayataccen kallo da ta lura yanabinta hip dinta da shi har da wani cewa ta kara tsayi hijjab dinta.

"babu a house shi isa."

Ta fada a fili tare da jan siririn tsaki.

Direct labour room tashige ba tare da ta koma office dinsu ba dan tasan tana shiga munafincin tmby ma ta isheta.



Kan doguwar sofa ya zauna tare da dafe Kansan da hannayensa biyu, amon muryarta ce kawai ke masa yawo a ka tare da gizon Mayan masakanta, iska mai karfi ya fuzgar tare da furta.

"what's wrong with me ne?"

Tsaye ya Mike tare da zarya cikin dakin, yinin ranar babu wani aiki da yayi har lkcn sallah yayi... 



2:30pm

Lokacin hibba tare da ramla suka fito daga ciKin asibitin,  suna tsaye bakin titi suna jiran mai napep ramla tace.

"kawa ban baki labari ba."

"bani nasha Allah yasa alkhairi ne."

Dai dai suka samu adai daitan suka shiga hibba tace.

"labarin me zaki bani."

"nafasa ai nasan koda na fada miki bazaki wani dauki abin serious ba,wai dazu ya kukayi da CMD ne?"

"dadina dake fa kenan ramla dan Allah ki fadan make going kibar mgnr CMD din nan mgnr dai da muka tattauna a meeting ce akan abubuwan da nafada."

"kinji dashi dai wannan halin naki hibba sae ke ynz nima bazaki iya fadan sirrinki ba, ai shikkenan mu dae muna murna bama cikin yan..."

"haba sis ramla wannan wata irin mgn kike kinsan dai bazanyi miki karya ba wllh tallahi babu mgnr da mukayi."

"toh yayi kyau hibbatullah."

Dariya hibba tayi tare da cewa.

"fadan labarin mana."

Dan gyara zama ramla tayi ramla irin manyan matan nan ne tana da kiba sosai kuma bata da tsayi sai taimakon takalmi, kawar hibba ce tun a school of nursing sai dai ta girmi hibba sosai domin zatayi 31yrs kuma itama budurwace tana da fada sosai gashi bata da hkr duk Wanda yayi mata sai tayi masa sae dai sam bata shiga sabgar mutum bare kayi mata taki kyaleka, jininta ya hadu da hibba sosai.

"wllh kawata wani sabon malami ne yazo Kano tundaga Niger jiya jiya ina fada miki yah ladi take fesan itama a asibitinsu taji ana mgnr sa ai hibba ina ganin lokacin dagawarmu yayi dan kinsan Allah yah ladi tace aikinsa kamar yankan wuka sha ynz magani yanzu."

"ohh kawata kawata wai dan Allah har sai yaushe zaku daina wahalar da kanku da asarar kudinku wajen wadannan kaskantun bayin wadanda wata kila kafisu daraja da matsayi a wajen ubangiji, ramla karfa ki manta cewa wadannan malaman shirka suke dan duk Wanda ze buga kasa yace ma ga abinda ze faru gobe mishirikine dan babu wanda yasan gaibu sai Allah, sstr na karki manta komai fa lokacine da aure da mutuwa duk da lokacinsu, wllh Allah ramla duk saurinmu sai munjira Allah, yayi ikonsa, ramla a kullum ina guje miki harka da wadannan bokayen."

"ahh hibba wllh ba bokaye bane."

"to menE idan ba bokaye ba ai duk wanda ze shiga lamarinka da ubangiji to bokane mushiriki ne, kuma wllh kudinku kawai sukeji dan basa wani aiki sae dea kuyi ta wahala kuna ciyar da katon banza, ramla ubangiji fa yace mu rokeki ze amsa mana me yasa bazamu tashi cikin dare mu daga hannayenmu sama muyi masa kuka tare da kirari mu rokeshi duk wata bukatarmu tabbas idan muka rokeshi ze amsa mana."

*"HIBBATULLAH* Allah yasa da alkhairi."

"haka zakice kenan?"

"to ya zance ustaziya, ke har kullum bakya ganewa ne amma zaki gane idan shekarunki suka kuma ja."

"bana rokan kowa sai Allah kuma insha allahu bazan ta6a rokan wani bayanshi ba, ramla zuwa wajen wani biyan bukata kamar fa kana kaiwa wannan mutumin karar ubangi..."

"ke hibba karkiyi mini sharri kuma ahh karki shiga tsakanina da ubangiji na."

Ta fada cikin fada.

"ba haka nake nufi ba ramla ina....."

Ringtone din wayarta ne ya katse mata mgn lalubar wayar dake jaka tayi tare da cirota Samsung s7 ce zabgegiya golden color mai kyau murmushi tayi tare da receiving wayar tace.

"assalamu alaikum ummata."

Shiru tayi kafin ta danyi dariya tace.

"haba ummana jiya ne kawai bankiraki ba kuma shima wllh babu kudi a wayar tawa ne."

Jim ta kumayi tana sauraron wayar kafin ta zaro ido ta dafe kirji tace.

"umma yau kuma amma..."

Katseta akayi daga daya 6angaren umma can Kuma ta sauke ajiyar zuciya tana son mayar da kwallar idonta tace.

"shikkenan umma gani nan insha Allah."

Daga hka ta datse kiran kallota ramla tayi kafin tace.

"sakon baba koh? "

Gyada mata kai kawai tayi fuskarta cike da damuwa. 

"ramla tace you see ynz sae ki koma kiji dame yazo kuma ki yarda muje gurin mutumin nan ya dan baki taimako kinki, dubarki ke ba wata mum
munar halitta gareki ba hasalima a ynz dae dakyar a samu mace mai kyan surarki da fuskarki, amma gashi babu ko Karen da ya ta6a shinshinaki sai dai yan iska gasu nan bila adadin duk bana auren, kizo muje muga me zece danni wllh ina zargin imma asiri akayi miki ko bakin aljani gareki mai hana aure."

"kibarshi idan har tanice wllh idan zan dawwama babu aure ramla bazanje wajen wani yayi mini abinda Allah beyi mini ba kuma ramla nasan kina kaunata kuma ngd sosai amma bAzani wajen kowa ba."

Baki sake ramla ke kallon ta kafin ta ta6e baki hadi da jijjiga kai tace.

"eh lallai gsky hibba da ace a zamani farko kikazo dakyar zaki karfi kalimatush shahada taurin kanki yayi yawa."

Shiru kawai tayi mata kafin tacewa mai napep din malam in kasamu waje saukeni anan na fasa zuwa jambulo din goron dutse zani."

Tsayawa yayi ta sauka tare da dauko 500 tace 

"dauki kudinka har da na kawata."

"da kinbari na biya da chaji a wajena "

*🅿 6*

Kiiiii kakeji aja wani mugun robust nan take mutanen gurin akayo kan motar dauke da salatin, wani mutum ya fito daga motar sanye da bakar coat hankali a tashe yana fadin.

"innalillahi WA'inna ilaihir'raji'un."

Ya tsugunna gaban hibba dake kwance cikin jini bata koda numfashi, mutanen gurin kowa ya dauka ta mutu, da sauri mutumin ya sungumeta yayi mota da ita zuwa lokacin kuka yake sosai wane karamin yaro yana furta "innalillahi wa'inna ilaihir'raji'un allahuma ajirni fil'musibatie wakhlif'ni khairan minna."

Jakarta da ta tarwatse gefe wani mutumi ya tattaro mata sai wayarta da ta rotse tayi filla filla, mutane biyu ne suka shiga motar suka tafi asibiti tun a motar mutumin ya kira headquarter din yan sanda dake hotoro yace a tura masa police asibitin standal ya taho da emergency accident.

Cikin yan mintoci suka karasa asibitin gadon tura mararsa lfy aka sakata tare da turata cikin sauri akayi emergency room da ita.

Duk wani cikecike da ya kamata barrister anwar yayi, likitoci sun fara kokarin ceto numfashinta domin suman da tayi sai kuma ciwukan da taji tare da kara guda a kafarta.

Har lokacin Barr anwar baya cikin nutsuwa fatansa kawai Allah yasa beyi kisan kai ba....



Tunda ya koma gida yakejinsa wani iri gabansa na wani irin faduwa damuwa da fargaba duk sun dameshi yanajin kamar wani abu na faruwa dashi ko yana shirin faruwa da shi dan shi bae ma ta6a shiga situation irin wannan ba tunda yake.

Sake relax yayi akan flat bed din dake gefan swimming pool din gida, daga shi sae boxer sae farar singlet farar fatar jikinsa sae wani glowing take ga wani kwantaccen gashi mai matukar kyau ajikinsa, idanuwansa a lumshe yana karanto duk wata addu'a da tazo bakinsa amma still bugun heartbeat dinsa daduwa yake.

Wayarsa ya janyo tare da danno number mum nashi da akayi saving da my happiness yayi calling bugu biyu mum ta dauka tare da fadin.

"happiness how are you?"

"not fine happiness."

Ya fada in baby voice.

"yah salam! What happen to you baby."

"my heartbeat."

Ya fada murya a raunane,  hankalin mum ya kuma tashi cikin harshen filatanci domin sometimes dashi takewa yaran nata mgn tun suna yara tace.

"me yake damun zuciyar taka baka da lfy ne? Ko dai matarka tayi ma wani Abu? "

"no mum just inajin zuciyana na bugawa gabana na faduwa like something is wrong."

kasa tayi da murya cikin kwantar da hankali tace.

"ayyah sorry happiness insha Allah babu abinda ze faru sai alkhairi daman haka rayuwar take watarana dadi wataran akasani haka kayi ta kiran sunan Allah kanayiwa annabi salati zakaji relief kaji."

"but mum bugun fa yayi yawa and... "

"haba only son my one and only babu abinda ze faru sai khair kaji, kaga duk muna lfy ni dad and ur sstrs so calm down babu abinda ze faru kaji habibi ko dai kanason ganin mum dinka ne yanzu ka ganni a Kano."

dan murmushi yayi tare da karajin kaunar mum din sosai da sosai yace. 

"yes mum domin already miss you."

"don't worry dear ina tare da kai a duk inda kake kuma ina maka Addu'a."

"thank you  I luv...."

Fizge wayar tayi da karfi tana daka masa wata irin harara idanuwanta gaba daya sun kada sunyi jazir,  tun dazu take tsaye kansa.

"ke bani wayata mana kina hauka ina waya da happiness din zaki fuzge."

"na fuzge din happiness din banza happiness din ho...."

Tas tas ya dauketa da maruka guda biyu Karo na farko a rayuwarsa da ya ta6a Marar wani, ko da kuwa kanwarsa najwa danshi mutun mai Marar fada.

"first and last zaki kara fizge waya A hannuna koda kuwa bada mum nake mgn ba idiot."

Daga haka ya fizge wayarsa tare da tureta gefe saura kiris ta fada pool din tayi saurin rike karfe, shi kuwa ransa a matukar 6ace ya shigewarsa ciki ba tare ma da ya kashe wayar da mum ba kuma duk abnd suke tana jinsu......




Duk suna tsatstsaye a tsakar gidan, unty ramla da baba da umma yah mudan da yah bappaliya sai kaninsu rayyan, duk suna tsaye tun dazu suke faman kiran wayarta a rufe.

unty ramla tayi ajiyar zuciya tare da fadin.

"hibba bata ta6ayin irin haka ba tatafi wani waje ba tare da ta sanar dani ba ko abban Yasir ina balle har ta kashe waya."

Rashida ce tashigo cikin gidan hankali tashe tayi sallama tare da fadin.

"har ynz ba'a samu nmbr tata ba koh? Ni wllh tun dazu muka rabu yh rabb yasa lfy dae."

"amin."

Yah mudan ya fada.

Baba yace. 

"wannan wane irin abune haka ace yarinya ta raina kowa ynz karfe nawa, duba 5 na yamma."

Kowa shiru yayi cike da jimami.



A hankali ta farka idanuwanta da sukayi mata nauyi wani irin ciwo kanta yake, duk jikinta ciwo yake ga kafarta dake matukar azaba domin ba'ayi daurin ba sbd kafar akwai ciwo ajiki sai ya warke.

Ido ta rintse da karfi duk abinda ya faru ya dawo mata, sake bude idonta Tayi a hankali tana saukesu a kansa, kallo daya tayi masa tare da rintse ido cike da azabar ciwon kai cikin rawar murya yace. 

"sannu baiwar Allah, Dan Allah kiyi hakuri wllh ban kula da shigowarki hanya ba dan Allah ki yafe mini."

Cikin rawar murya tace.

"kai na."

Dasauri ya fita daga dakin yana fadin."

"let's me col d doctor."

daga haka ya fice da sauri, doctor ya kira tazo ta dubata tare da yi mata allura.

*🅿 7*

kashe kiran yayi tare da duba missed calls na wayarsa yaga an kirkirashi da dama ciki har da granny dinsa wadda ta kira sama da sau ashirin ita ya fara dannawa kira yana dariya.

"oh granny na manta fa Ashe ban sanar miki ba, tafiyace ta sake kamani dazu bashiri zuwa Abuja yanzu hakama kin ganni a kwance."

"Abuja dan kaniyarka yaushe ka dawo ma da har zaka sake komawa toh yasin maza ka tattaro ynz ka dawo gida tunkafin naci mutuncinka. "

"nop granny kalau nake, dazu a hanyata ta dawowa tsotsayi yasa na ka...."

"ka kashewa? Kana hauka ne na lalace yau salamatu uwarwa ka kashe garin shegen gudun nan naka da na hanaka salihu gashi kajanyowa kanka azumi sittin kai da ba cikakkiyar lfy ba wayyo ni."

dariya sosai yake kyalkyala mata ganin ita bata tsaya taji Karshen mgn ba amma gashi duk tabi ta ishi mutane da koke koke har ta fara lissafin kaffara ka tseshi tayi cikin kuka tace.

"au dan ubanka ba marigayi ba shine Kake dariya ko wato Kai ko ajikinka ko? Ynz nawa sukace abiyasu diyya na kirawo baturiyar uwarka dan wllh ita zata biya dan tafika kudi, azumin kuma maje mu nemi fatawa mai sauki ko ciyarwa ne ayi."

Sosai yake dariya har da hawaye ya rufe bakinsa gudun kar hibba ta farka yayi kamar zeyi kuka yace.

"granny ae wadanda suka mutun su ashirin ne."

Sai kuwa ta sake fasa ihu tana fadin.

"wayyo mungama shiga uku mun lalace 1200 zakayi azumi wllh bazaka iya ba dangi ta hau dan kowa sai ya dauki wanda zeyi. "

"mtsw matsalata dake kenan ke har abada bazaki ta6a tsayawa kiji karshen mgn ba sai ki hau kururuwa kina cikawa mutane kunne wa yace miki wani na kashe?"

nan ya fada mata duk abnd ya faru, sosai taji tausayin hibba tare dayi mata addu'ar samun lfy.

Yana zaune a main parlour yana wani aiki a system dinsa, gefansa cup din coffee ne da yasa house help din gidan ta hado masa domin tun safe babu abnd ya sawa cikinsa.

"shut up stupid Ashe baka da mutunci zaka iya fadawa uwarka Amina wannan mgnr? Har kai tsiya doctor dan tsohun shugaban NGO ka Mari my only daughter baka isa ba wllh inba dan ina jin nauyi da girma na ubanka ba wllh danasa an daure mini Kai me akayi akayika ko dan kana takama da soyayyar da take maka, Kai sanin kanka kasan Fina she's not ur mate, to first nd last time karka kuskura ka sake attempt din dukar mini diya dan bakasan ciwon haihuwa nonsense mtsw."

Kit ta datse kiran, idanuwana ya sauka fuskar mahir da duk jarabata na sakegano ainihin halin da yake ciki, illa iyaka dai naga ya zubawa wayar ido baya ko kiftawa, daga karshe naga ya girgiza kansa tare da ajiye wayar ya cigaba da aikin gabansa, can kuma ganin ze fara shirme sai ya ture laptop din tare da jingina kansa kan royal chair din da yake zaune ya daura hannayensa gaba daya bayan keyarsa tare da lumshe kyawawan eyes dinshi, babu abnd yake Maimaitawa sai sunan Allah a zuciyarsa sbd bayason yayi Abu cikin fushi, amma duk inda 6acin rai yake to nasa ya 6aci babban abnd yafi kona ransa shine kararsa da takai gurin mahaifiyarta, yau shekararsu biyar da aure duk irin zaman kuncin da yake ciki da ita da duk laifikan da takeyi masa ko kusa bai ta6a sanarwa da kowa ba amma sae gashi shi kawai dan ya mareta akan zagin mahaifiyarsa da tayi ta sanar da uwarta itama da yake uwace Marar ilimin addini shine zatabi zancen yarta har tayi masa cin mutunci irin wannan ya tabbata ba yabon kai ba mum dinsa da iyakarta diploma bazata aikata abinda mace kamar mum din Fina wadda take professor tayi ba, tabbas akwai tarin bambanci tsakanin ilimin Boko Dana addini zakaga mace har mace Mai tarin wayewa da krtn Boko amma wllh jahilace abnd zatayi ko yar secondary school bazatayi ba, sbd ita babu abinda ta sani sai Boko.
Lallai mata da yawa yan Boko mararsa ilimin addini basu da hnkl, jinjina Kai yayi yana sake tunano irin drummer da kullum suke da dad dinsa akan haihuwa amma da wasa bai ta6a sanar da dashi cewa ita finan ce da kanta tayi tsarin iyali ba, a kullum fada masa yake Allah ne bai kawoba.

"hmm, nafisa banason na aikata abinda zeyi sanadin tarwatsewar rayuwarki ko dan darajar dumbin kaunar da kike mini amma yanzu tura ta kai bango."

Ya fada a fili tare da mikewa ya rufe laptop dinsa ya dauki wayarsa ya nufin upstairs din....







*******


A farfajiyar asibitin yah mudan yayi parking motar sa umma da baba da rayyan da unty ramla suka fito shi yah baffaliya ya wuce gida an masa waya matarsa ba lfy. 

Wayar Barr anwar suka kira suka sanar masa da zuwan su room no din dakin yabasu suka karaso cikin sauri.

Umma ce ta fara shigowa dauke da sallama kallo daya zakayi mata kasan Tasha kuka har ynz barcin take domin allurar mai nauyi akayi mata, duk tsayawa sukayi akanta suna kallon irin raunikan da taji sae dea fuskarta babu abnd ya sameta, rayyan yakai hannu zae ta6a kafarta Barr yayi saurin cewa. 

"sorry bro karka ta6a da akwai karaya a wajen."

kirji umma ta dafe tare da zaro ido tace 

"abun babbane kenan har da karaya Allah sarki yarinyar kirki."

"ya za'ayi abar yarinya da karaya ba tare da an dauraba tun dazu ko sai kafar tagama tsami."

Barrister anwar yace. 

"sorry bro karkayi saurin jin haushina tsotsayine yana kan kowa kuma bada ganganci nayi mata haka ba kaddarace da Allah yariga da ya ru...."

Cikin tsawa yah mudan yace. 

"zaka fadan kaddarane? Fina saninta kAyi?"

"kar na sake jin bakinka muddasir."

Baba ya fada a takaice kafin ya sake kallon Barr din da duk jikinsa yayi sanyi kansa a kasa idanuwansa har sun fara ciccikowa da hawaye yace.

muryarsa na rawa yace. 

"baba doctor NE yace babu damar ayi mata dauri yanzu sbd ciwukan dake kafar dole sai ciwon da taji ya bushe ya kama jikinsa nan da sati sannan za'ayi mata daurin. "

"Allah sarki hibbatullah Ashe zaki sha wahala, tun kina yarinya kike fama da tsotsayi iri iri yh ubangaji Allah ya baki lfy ya cire miki azaba da zafin ciwon jikinki."

kowa na dakin jikinsa yayi sanyi dajin sai nan da 1wk za'a daurata, unty ramla itama sai da tayi kuka,shi kuwa Barr zube gwiwoyinsa yayi a kasa yana rokon suyi hkr a lkcn sukace kaddara ce babu wanda bata Kai godia sukayi masa ma akan abnd yayi dan da wanine ma guduwa zeyi, sai dai yah mudan har ynz bai bar jin zafinsa ba.

Fita yayi yaje wani katon shopping mall yayi masu siyayya mai tarin yawa tare da magunguna.

*🅿 9*

After one week's 

Lokacin jikin hibba har yayi sauki sosai har an daura kafar sun dawo gida, yayin da shakuwa sosai tashiga tsakaninta da Barr anwar da granny dinta, kusan kullum sai yazo kuma yana jumawa sosai da yake mutum ne mai barkwanci da raha shi isa hatta da umma duk Sun saba da bada dariyarsa, 6angare guda kuwa na zuciyarsa cike yake da soyayyar hibba.


6angaren CMD kuwa gaba daya komai ya rincha6e masa bashi da aiki sai tunanin hibba, duk daren Allah cikin mafarkinta yake, da kaganshi ya zama so silent hatta da mum ta lura da hakan Dan ynz idan ya kirata basu fiya dadewa suna mgn ba, idan ma ta jashi da hira uhm um ce kawai amsar,  batason matsa masa da tambaya shi isa bata fiye takurashi ba, yayin da madam Fina ko a kasan takalminta da chanzawarsa domin ita taci uwarsa miskilanci da  girman Kai, hasalima ita fushima take da shi domin tun ranar da suka sami sa6ani be kuma bi ta ita ba, gurin aiki ma sun Deana fita tare kowa tafiyarsa yake, sai dai duk wani motsinsa akan idonta yake ana kawo mata rahoto. 



Yau yana zaune cikin office dinsa sanye cikin wasu disgner suit farare tas fuskarsa sai wani glowing take na kyau, amma kallo daya zakayi masa kasan cewa yana cikin damuwa. 

Mtsw yadanja tsaki kafin ya mike yana rike da wayansa, hatta da watch din hannunsa abin kallone yarda yake wani kyalli da kaganshi kasan akwai tsada. 

Fita yayi tare da barwa PA dinsa office din, cikin takunsa na isa ya fara takawa farfajiyar asibitin fuskarsa kwata kwata babu walwala, hannayensa gaba daya zube cikin aljihun wandonsa har ya isa ga 6angaren 'yan haihuwa. 

Babbar theater cike taf da masu na kuda wani tsawa rabi bukar ta dakawa wani mutumi da yake a hargitse matarsa na zubar da jini tace.

"baka da hnkl zaka shigo mana haka, bakasan da matan mutane bane ko Kai jakine."

Mutumin ya kalleta gwanin ban tausayi yace. 

"kiyi hkr baiwar Allah bata iya tafi ita...."

"dallah gafara can ina ruwan wani da rashin tafiyarta ubanwa yayi mata cikin, dallah malam cikata tayi tafiya."

Jikinsa in banda rawa babu abinda take wata mata tace. 

"ke kuwa yar nan kamar ba mace ba ki duba halin da matar nan take kice a saketa, taya zata iya takawa."

"ohh zaki nuna mini aikina kenan, okay come zoki zauna kiyi naki tsarin yar iya."

Matar a fusace tace.

"amma yarinyar nan baki da kunya ni zaki dubi tsabar idona ki fada mini wannan mgnr wllh ki guji bakin duniya, aikin da kuke ba hauka bane anan aljanar ku take."

Khady da tafito tace.

"to idan ku kuke bada aljana basai ku hana mu shigaba. "

Duk abinda suke akan idanun CMD suke ransa a matukar 6ace ea besan wannan bura uban suke aikatawa ba, cikin isa ya tako tare da turo wata wheel chair sae lkcn hatta da staff din wajen suka kula da shi, nan take jikinsu ya hau rawa gaban matar nan ya ajiye tare da kallon mijin yayi masa umarnin ya ajiyeta da kansa ya tura ta har cikin labour room din, gaba daya jikin ma'aikata gurin kowa yayi sanyi cikin hegu ya duri ruwa🤣🤣🤣

ba takan wadda ya sake bi da kansa ya kula masu haihuwar lokacin har azuhr yana abu daya, kafin Yafito agajiye sosai,  har lkcn gurin tsit kakeji sae dea ihun masu haihuwa, da yafito wani toilet yashiga tare da dan gyara jikinsa ya fito, main  office nasu ya shiga tare da zama, hatta da staff duk suna wajen, ruster ya bukaci abashi nan ya fara dubawa yana kiransu one by one masu mrng duty amma abin mamaki rabinsu basuzo ba, gyada kai yayi sannan ya fara fada cikin kausasshiyar murya kyakkyan warning yayi masu tare da gargadi, wadanda basuzo ba ya bukaci a turo masa gobe.




Duk suna zaune a kan tabarman da aka shimfida tsakar gidan, Barr anwar zaune ya baje yanacin dan wake, yayin da hibba take zone ta daura kafarta kan wani pillow sai umma itama tana zaune, dariya suke ta kyalkyalawa cike da farinciki shi kuma sai zuba masu labari yake.

*🅿 10*

"toh umma sai kindawo."

"kamar da kai ake, ummata sai kindawo plz ki tahon da dambun gidan suna."

"ni dai umma na gidan suna nakeso."

Banza umma tayi mata sannan ta fice, baki ta sake turowa dashi ai kuwa Barr anwar ya tuntsire da dariya har da sunkuyawa, ya dago ya Kalle ya kuma bushewa da dariya, rasa abinda zata bugeshi tayi sae ta fara hawayen dariyar da yake mata, ganin tana hawaye yasashi nutsuwa tare da gyara zama cikin salon rarrashi yace.

"ba kaine Kake mini dariya ba Ka zamar dani gurguwa kuma ka tasani gaba da dariya kamar mahaukaciya."

"hibba yaki ya saurareni tun a waje mutanen unguwa suka sanar masa da fitarki, ko da ya dawo fada kawai da masifa ya hauni da ita yaki yabari nayi mgn wai ni a yau mahaifinku ze bude baki yace nike hanaki kiyi aure idan na lalata masa tarbiyyar 'ya Allah ya isa beyafe ba ni."

Umma ta fada Tana sakin kuka, rayyan yace. 

"gsky Allah ya tsinewa munafukan unguwa ace mutum baka taka Masa ba bashi da gadonka amma kullum idonsa akan ka keco da irin wannan masifar ta unguwannin mu, umma dan Allah kiyi hkr ku daina kukan haka."

Hibba tace.

"rayyan dan Allah ni nake da iko da rayuwata zan dauki kaina nakai gidan mijine? Rayyan zamanin nan wllh samun miji abune mai wahala ya zansa raina."

hannunta ya riko yace. 

"unty hibba kibar kuka ki daina damuwa insha Allah jinkirinki alkhairi ne Allah ze baki mijin da zakiji dadinsa hatta da dangi da masu samiki ido zasuji dadin mijinki daga karshe kuma suji kunya."

Kada kai tayi tana share hawayenta, shi isa take matukar son rayyan domin mutum ne da ya iya mgn, umma ma share hawayenta tace rayyan ya kuma cewa. 

"kije ki bawa baba hakuri."

umma tace.

"tabarshi zuwa gobe da safe dan ynz ko tayi masa mgn baze saurareta ba."

To sukace sukayi shiru kowa da abnd ke ransa.

Ita dae hibba tayi hkr zata kar6i anwar ko dan ta huta da halin da take ciki sai sam batajin koda dingon son anwar zuciyarta da ruhinta babu wanda suke gani face mahir tana masa wani irin makahon So wanda tsaf zata iya loosing rayuwarta idan ta rasa shi.





Sai daf da magriba suka dawo gida har zuwa ynz ko uffan bata shiga tsakanin mahir da Fina ba miskilaci + Girman kai =shariya🤣🤣.

Anan masallacin gaban gidansu ya sauka ita kuma aka karasa da ita ciki, zama tayi bisa parlour tana sauke numfashi mai aiki ta kwalawa kira tace ta kawo mata ruwa wata bura uba da nagani fans wai hatta da shoe wannan mai aikince ta cirewa fina🙁

Wayara
ta ta fara ringtone number secret security dinta ta Gani cikin sauri tayi picking call din tare da fadin.

"yh."

Daga daya 6angaren cikin harshen turanci yace. 

"how far ma'am."

"fyn yh ne."

"ma'am da big problem wllh I Ned talk to you now."

Cikin tsari har tana kwarewa tace.

"lafiya?"

"ba lao ba ma'am just can I see you? "

Dasauri tace.

"yes yes, meet me at 212 yanzu yanzu."

Daga haka ta datse kiran ta mike ko takalmi babu a kafarta gyalenma a hannu ta dauka sai wayarta ta fice da gudu, wani mugun kira take kwallawa driver da har ya kai raka'ar karshe ta sallah yayi saurin sallamewa ya iso gabanta da gudu tare da tsugunnawa. 

A hargitse tace.

"bani Carkey."

Gaba daya ya rude ya fara lalube wata uwar ashariya ta sakar masa yayi saurin fito da key din ya bata motar da suka dawo da ita ta shiga daman tuni mai gadi ya wangale gate tun kiran da tayiwa driver yasan fita ce, da mugun speed taja motar kamar zata tashi sama driver ya sauke wata nannauyar ajiyar zuciya yace. 

"matsiyaciya karewar sauri ki kamo gobe ma jarababbiya ni ynz gashi ta 6atan sallah tasa sai na sake sabuwa daman ita ba damunta tayi ba."

Mai gadi ya bushe da dariya yace. 

"ai wllh ban ta6a ganin yar akuya ba kamar matar nan gaba daya bata da hankali."

"shegiya mai siffar yan wuta ba."

Wata dariyar mai gadi ya Saki harda rike ciki yace. 

"kai dai kaje ka sake sallaharka. "

Tsaki yaja kafin ya tada kabbara. 


ikon Allah ne kawai ya kai Fina 212 domin wani mahaukacin gudu Da take shararawa ba ruwanta da wanda ke gabanta kawai tukwi take. 

ko da ta isa har Samuel ya iso yana zaune yana shan lemo tana isowa tayi masa wani banzan kallo tace.

"har kana da tym din shan lemo OK fada mini meke faruwa."

"ma'am dazu a green pack ko kinga abinda ya faru?"

"taya zan sani."

"I mean kin kula da wata budurwa da ta fito tare da wani gaye har mood din da yarinyar ta shiga."

"mtsw and so what, kaga ka fadan make faruwa."

Tsaye ya Mike tare da fadin.

"I thinking wannan yarinyar da oga suna da good relation maybe suna soyay...... "

Tas tas tas, kakejin ta daukeshi da maruka masu axaba tace.

"kana hauka ne kwayar da kakesha har tayi attracting maka hauka, baka da hnkl wannan shine aikin da nasaka kazo ka fadan mijina yana da good relation da wata banace ka kashe ko wacce tayi gigin shiga tsakanina da Dr Mahir ba ba haka nace maka ba!!!"

Ta fada cikin karaji tana shan kwalarsa wane mahaukaciya. 

"sorry MA.... "

"Quite I said keep quiet stupid idiot bakasan aikinka ba Samuel amma na baka nan da 24hr inason sani wace wannan yarinyar kafin kuma Ka kashe mini ita dan wllh babu macen da ta isa tashiga tsakanin *NI DA DR MAHIR* zanyi maka transfer na 50000 ka samo mini ita wace understand."

Kasa yayi da kai tare da sara mata, rai a matukar 6ace ta fice daga wajen fuuuu. 





Yana zaune a parlour ta shigo ko sallama babu mayafinma ta yadashi a hanya kamar mahaukaciya tayi fuu tajiyo muryarsa a nutse yace.

"daga ina kike?"

A fusace ta juyo daman a wuya take tace.

"daga inda ka aikeni mtsw...."

Taja tsaki tare da hayewarta upstairs abinda.

*🅿 13*

Girgiza kai kawai yayi zuciyarsa nayi masa d'aci, hmmm Fina ki guji fushin namiji mai hakuri. 


Washe gari.

Da sassafe mum din Fina ta kirata, Fina da bata samu wani cikakken barci ba jiya sae ynz cikin bacci ta daga wayar domin ta gane ringtone din mum dinta ne.

"hello good morning mummy."

"mrng dear how are you? "
"fyn nd you?"

"same dear,  daman kiranki nayi naji yaushe zaki tahau abj kinsan gobe ne tafiya tamu."

Ya mutsa face tayi tace.

"mummy wacce tafiya kuma?"

"haba dear harkin manta zaki rakani Cyprus medical check."

"oh mummy na manta, gsky mum ban fadawa mahir ba kinsan...."

Katseta tayi a tsawace tace.

"dallah rufan baki mahir din me."

Baki ta turo tace.

"ko banza dai he's my husband yana da right da ze san...."

"karki 6atan rai dan ubanki kinji dai abinda na fada miki yanzu jet zezo daukarki so be ready ok."

Bata bari taji abinda zata fada ba ta katse wayar jifa Fina tayi da wayar ta koma tayi kwanciyarta. 


Karfe 10am

Dai dai jet ya tsaya gaban gidan Dr mahir, lokacin yana zaune balcony dake saman gidan yana kallon rabar gidan gabansa laptop ce yana aiki daga shi sai fararar jallabiya mai gajeran hannu, duk a tunaninsa mum din Fina ce tazo amma ga mamakinsa sai ganin Fina yayi ta fito sanye da wata dakakkiyar gown mai tsananin kyau da daukar ido, tayi rolling kanta da mayafi taci wani no respect wanda yayi mata kyau sosai,  tsaye mahir ya Mike yana kallon ikon Allah da mamakinsa sai gani yayi ta fice daga gidan tana waya pilot ya bude mata jet din ta shige ba tare da 6ata lokaci ba sukayi sama.

Lumshe idanuwansa yayi ransa na wani irin 6aci tabbas Fina ta riga da tagama Raina shi, me za'ayi da auren mace Marar ilimin addini, da yake weekend ne babu aiki yasa shi mikewa ya shige bedroom dinsa ransa duk babu dadi.

Karar message dinta ne ya shigo wayarsa be budeba bare ya duba ransa ya kuma 6aci.





Tana tashi da safe wanka kawai tayi ta shirya cikin rigar material Marar nauyi tana zaune gefan gadonta ta tsunduma cikin tunanin wayarta tayi kara alamar shigowar sako dauka tayi ta duba taga anwar ne sako na ashirin kenan da ya turo mata daga jiya zuwa ynz dubawa tayi taga yasa.

_"I feel something in my heart, it's like a little flame, every time I see you this flame lights up, this flame is special for you, because I LOVE YOU gwd mrng....!"_

Tausayinsa taji ya sake kamata tabbas ya riga ya gama fadawa cikin kogin kaunarta, wayar ta tsurawa ido sai ga kiransa ya shigo sai da ta kusa katsewa ta daga cikin siririyar muryarta tace.

"salamu alaikum."

Yana kwance a katafaren Italian bed dinsa jin muryarta yasa ya sake wani relax yana rukunkume pillow yace. 

"baby kintashi shine baki kirani ba koh? "

Lumshe ido tayi ta sake budewa tace.

"yanxu na tashi ina kwana?"

"Kintashi lfy?"

"alhmdllh."

"yah kafar taki?"

"dasauki."

Shiru sukayi kowa na sauraron yarda numfashin junansu ke fita, cikin whisper yace. 

"angle when zan turo magabatana dan gsky banson dogon relationship."

"unhm da wuri haka."

"tome zamu jira angle dagani harke ba yara bane so banga amfanin mu zauna muna dating mai tsayi."

"mubi komai a hankali dai karmu saka gaggawa a lamuran mu musamman na aure, tunda sha'anine mai girma."

Shiru yayi yana sauraran yarda take fitar da magana daki daki soyayyarta na sake kamashi. 

Muryar umma ce take kwalla mata Kira, yace.

"umma ke kiranki?"

"eh."

"okay Bari na ajiye wayar sae anjima."




Monday

2:30am 

Rashida ta shigo gidan su hibba, lokaci umma ce kawai a kitchen tana juye abinci a cula.

"umma sannu da gida ina yini."

"ah ah rashida kece a gidan namu, ya aikin."

"alhmdllh wllh umma hibba fa?"

"tana ciki bacci take inaji dan naji tace kanta ke dan mata ciwo."

"ayyah bari naje."

Ta fada tana shiga dakin hibba, tana kwace sanye take da vest da zani gashin kanta mai yalwa da tsayi duk ya baje kan pillow zama gefanta rashida tayi ta
[9/22, 2:41 PM] Ummee Garkuwa: *🅿 13.......continue*


Ta saka hannu ta dan ta6a kafarta Marar ciwon, dan motsawa tayi tana ya mutsa fuska taga rashida tace.

"sister kece da ranar nan."

"dole ki ganni ai hibba tashi, da akwai matsala fa."

Zaune hibba ta Mike tana daura dan kwalinta tace.

"lafiya dai koh?"

"ina ta lfy sister,  wai daman baki sanar da rashin lfyrki a department ba."

"bangane ban sanarba, nayi latter na aikawa h. O. D har nan ma yazo dubani."

Gumin da tun dazu rashida keyi ta share da hijjabinta da ta cire yar karamar jelar kitsonta ta bayyana tace.

"to munafuncin department har da ke dan dazu HOD ya kirani office, ya bani wannan takadar ya jaddada mini cewa na tabbatar na baki takardar nan sako daga CMD ina kyautata zaton Query letter ce."

Dim dim gaban hibba ya fada tabi takardar da rashida ke miko mata da kallo tace.

"innalillahi WA'inna'ilaihir'raji un ni wannan wata irin musifa ce mai ya hadani da Query."

"munafunci mana, kinsan  kwanaki CMD yazo ya kar6i register tun case din su rabi lawan, to ya bada Query da yawa wllh bama iya department din mu har da sauran department ke har doctors ya bawa wasun su, ina kyautata zaton wata bakar munafukarce zata zame taje ta fada masa wani abun."

Wata zazzafar kwalla ta zubowa hibba tasa hannu ta share cikin sanyin murya tace.

"duk mai bina da sharri kansan ze koma insha Allah."

"kinga hibba duk ba wannan ba yanzu abinda zamuyi shine dole fa gobe ki samu ki shiga asibiti ki samu kiga CMD kuyi magana akan matsalar nan, kinga wannan takardar kibar rainata dan wllh daga ita sai kora danni da abani wannan shegiyar abar gwanda ayi mini transfer a kaini doguwa."

"insha Allah goben zani zan fadawa yah anwar sai ya kaini."

"toh shikkenan hakan ma yayi kuje goban, sannan nima xan zuba ido a cikin department ubangiji yasa na gane aciki wata shegiyar takai munafuncin nan humm zataci uwarta wllh."

Umma ta shigo dauke da flat din abinci, tace.

"rashida ga abinci."

"Kai umma har da wahala haka ni da zan tafi ma."

"ki dai ci abinci keda kika dawo daga aiki."

Hibba cikin sanyi murya tace.

"umma kinga takardar gargadi da aka aikon daga CMD na asibitin mu."

Ido umma ta zaro tare da dafe kirji tace.

"subhanallahi mai kikayi toh?"

Rashida da takai loma bakinta tace.

"umma ki kwantar da hnkln ki plz bafa wata matsala bace, munafunci ne kawai abun ke dai kiyi mata addu'a Dr mahir bashi da Matsala ko kadan da taje ta sameshi komai zeyi dai dai."

Ajiyar zuciya umma ta saki tace.

"toh Allah ya kiyaye."

Daga haka ta fice daga dakin.

Rashida kuma ta cigaba da cin abincinta ita kuwa hibba shiru kawai tayi tana bin takardar da kallo ba tare da ta bude ba.




Karfe goma na dare bayan sun gama waya da Barr anwar,  har tayi addu'a ta kwanta sai ta mike tare da kunna fitilar wayarta ta janyo Jakarta tare da dauko takardar da rashida ta kawo mata haka kawai ta tsinci kanta da faduwar Gaba har ta ajiye takardar sai kuma ta bude good handwriting dinsa ya bayyana a takardar rubutu kamar haka:-

_dear future wife_
_no poems no fancy words I just want the world to know that I LOVE YOU my princess with all my heart._
_your hair is black like the darkest night, your eyes beautiful like the finest piece of art, your slightest touch makes me lose all my worries, that is why it can only be your that is meant for me_
_GWS habebbty_

Ta karanta letter din nan yafi karfin sau hamsin wani irin dadi da zulumi ne ya kamata, special digital dinshi ta gani a kasa ta manta da dare tayi sauri sawa cikin wayarta jikinta na rawa ta danna Kira, lokacin yana parlour yana karanta Alkur'ani mai girma wayarsa dake silent yaga ta kawo haske tsurawa wayar ido yayi har ta katse wani kiran ya sake dawowa, a hnkl yayi picking tare da karawa a kunnensa cikin sanyin murya yace.

"salam, who in the line."

Daga chan 6angaren hibba ta zame ta kwanta saman pillow tana lumshe ido murya a matukar sanyaye tace.

"Hibbatullah..... "

*🅿 14*

Wata sanyayyar ajiyar zuciya ya saki wadda har sai da hibba ta jiyota cikin whisper yace. 

"lovely name for a beautiful owner."

Shiru ko wannensu yayi kafin ya katse kiran, Bin wayar tayi da kallo tana imagining fuskar mahir mai dauke da annuri, murmushinsa mai kyau take hangowa tana wannan tunanin ya kirata, kamar me jira tayi saurin daga tare da karawa a kunne.

"yah jikin naki."

Taji ya fada atakaice.

"dasauki."

Ta furta a hankula, shiru ya danyi kafin yace. 

"hibba my heart love with you tun time din da na fara ganinki, na rasa sukuni nashiga damuwa akanki naji kishinki lokaci guda sai daga baya zuciya ta fada mini sonki take so bana wasa ba kuwa, kece macen da mum dita ta dade tana mini addu'ar samu domin yau na tabbatar da haka saboda kyakkyawar shedar da kika samu a wajen jama'ar dake tare da ke, hibbatullah wllh tallahi I love you nayi miki alkawarin farinciki marar musaltuwa a rayuwar auren mu zan zama bawanki zanyi miki hidima har karshen numfashina plz accept me hibba."

Ya karasa mgnr cikIn alamar roko.

Hibba kuwa gaba daya kalamansa Sun gama ratsa jini da jijiyoyin jikinta wani irin farinciki ne ke ratsata ga wani sanyi da taji zuciyarta nayi ji take kamar an yaye mata duk wani zunubanta yau wai itace professional Dr like Mahir Abdul'Mu'in ke furta mata kalmar so,  kai alhmdllh burinta ya gama cika. 

"habebbty kinyi shiru plz accept me."

Kasa kasa ta sakeyi da murya tace.

"I will need a time to think about it."

Shiru ya danyi kafin yace. 

"ok no problem but when zan samu amsa na."

"1month."

Kwarewa yayi yana tari tace.

"what?"

"ke kikasa na kware love 30days fa kinaso kafin time din ace miki na mutu kenan, gaskiya 1month yayi yawa ina laifin 3days shine ka'ida."

Gutun murmushi ta danyi sannan tace.

"Allah ya kaimu."

"amin love me kikeyi ne haka?"

"ina kwance."

"OMG! Dama munyi aure muna tare manne da juna muna hirarmu mai dadi."

"hmm, bari na kwanta good night."

"Kai love kina korani."

Ya fada kamar ba Dr mahir da ta sani ba.

"nop inajin bacci ne."

"kk habebbty sleep well kiyi addu'a before sleep I love you too."

Daga haka ya datse kiran.

wata kara hibba ta Saki tare da cusa kanta cikin katifa cike da tarin farincikin da bata ta6ajin irinsa a rayuwa ba, komai tana ganinsa kamar a mafarki ne.

"awww...."

Ta fada tana sake gyara kwanciyarta,  a ranar dai dakyar ta samu tayi bacci sbd tsabagen tunanin mahir kwana tayi tana mafarkinsa,  shima hakan take a 6angarensa domin shi har wankan tsarki yayi da asuba.


Washe gari shine ya fara kiranta suka gaisa, tana mamakin ina jarababbiyar matarsa take har ya samu damar waya, ji tayi ynz kwata kwata bata tsoron haduwa da Fina, anwar ma ya kirata kamar yarda ya saba.



Ana gobe kwana ukun da tabawa mahir ze cika, tana zaune a parlour tana taya umma gyara wake, yaro yazo yace ana sallama da hibba ido hibba ta zaro tace.

"ni kuma injiwa? "

Umma ta kalleta tace.

"meye abun rudewa haka kuma, kai yaro jeka kace injiwa."

Yaron yace inji anwar wai.

"toh anwar sabon salo yau kuma baze shigo ba, kwana biyu na fuskanci kamar akwai wani abu tsakaninku."

"hmmm ummata kenan, babu komai tsakanin mu."

"gulmammiya idan tayi tsami naji, ki tashi kin ajiye shi waje."

Mikewa tayi domin ynz kafarta ta warke sumul sai dan dingisata da take kadan, hijjab dinta ta saka sai yar powder da ta shafa ta fesa turare ta fito, ta kalli umma da tayi mata kallo daya ta ta6e dariya tayi ta fita tana fadin.

"ummanta ta kaina."

Tsaye ta ganshi a bakin motarsa, yana sanye da sky blue din shadda da bakar dara yayi kyau sosai.

Murmushi ta sakar masa ta shima ya mayar mata karasawa tayi gaban motar ta tsaya tace.

"ina yini."

"kinyi lfy shugabar kyawawa."

Dan murmushi tayi tace.

"yah su granny?"

"tana gaidaki, zuwa nayi muyi sallama tafiya ta kamani zuwa Abuja yanzu ynzu."

"Abuja kuma da yamman nan kabari sai da safe mana karkayi tafiyar dare."

"ai bani kade bane, kuma a jirgi zamu tafi ynz haka ma ni kade suke jira nace bazan tafiba sai nazo nayi sallama da ke kuma naga kyakkyawar fuskanki."

Murmushi tayi tana dan rufe fuskarta, hira suka danyi kadan kafin sukayi sallama, kudi yabata amma fafur taki kar6a daga karshe gida tashige da sauri ta barshi. 


Tana komawa ciki, wayarta na kara umma tace.

"keni jarabar wayar nan taki tun dazu ake aikin kiranki dauka kiji."

Dasauri ta dauki wayar ganin mai kiranta ta shige daki, umma tabi bayanta da kallo kwana biyu ta rasa wane irin nishadi 'yarta ta take ciki.

Dole ta sanar da ramla domin itace abokiyar shawarar hibba ita take sanarwa da komai nata, Tana bukatar ta bincika mata abnd ke faruwa da hibba.

Hijjab din jikinta ta cire tana kwanciya kan katifarta tare dayin rufda ciki, cikin sanyayyar murya mai zaki tace.

"assalamu alaikum."

"wslm, love ina kika ajiye wayar ne tun dazu nake kira baki receiving ba har kinsa hawan ruwana ze tashi."

Yar dariya ta Saki tace.

"bana kusa da wayar ne ykk yh gida."

"nop fine."

"why? "

"bcoz I miss you."

"hmm! "

Hira sukayi ta sha har aka fara kiran sallah magriba kafin suyi sallama.

*🅿 15*

Dr Fina ce tsaye gaban wani tankameman dressing mirror mai girman gsk wanda yake shake taf da costumes iri daban daban na kwalliya, daure take da wani dan 6ingilin towel red color da alama daga wanka ta fito dan kanta ajike yake, tsayawa tayi tana karewa kanta kallo, cikin zuciyarta tace magana, tabbas ita kyakkyawace tana da kyan da tafi karfin ko wanne namiji ya wulakanta ta, amma me yasa mahir yake nuna halin ko in kula a kanta, dan yaga ina sonshi da yawa, tabbas da soyayyar mahir aka halicceni kuma da ita zan koma kuma shima ae yana sona sai dai nasan baya yimini kwatan kwacin son da nake masa, hummm! Ta sauke numfashi mummy ce ta shigo cikin shiga ta alfarma da ka ganta kasan mahaifiyar Fina ce domin kamarsu daya sak hatta da girman jikinsu daya sai dai da kaga mummy kasan gogaggiyar Boko ce number 1 wadda daga yanayin jikinta kasan Hutu da rayuwar jindadi sun zaunar mata.

"Fina na dauka tuni kin gama shiryawa tundazu ke muke jira muyi dinner."

Baki fina da dan ta6e tace.

"Anya mummy zan iya cin wani Abu."

"dallah malama me kikaci tun da kikazo gidan, idan akwai abinda ke damunki ki fadan mana am ur mom baki da wata da zakiyi making shawara sama dani."

Zamewa fina tayi ta zauna kan stool ta dubi mummyn ta ta da kyau tace.

"mummy nice kuwa mai matsala tunda har ynz bangama tabbatar da cewa mahir nawa bane ni kade."

"mtsw! Wllh tunda nake ban ta6a ganin mayyar 'ya ba kamarki nafisa, kamar ba jinina ba dan ubanki meye wani mahir yadda kike kaunar yaron nan ko ubanki bakya kauna haka baki da magana sai mahir mahir, namiji dai ba abin goyo bane wllh daga lkcn da kika nuna ke shi kadaine a gabanki lokacin ze bude kafa yayi ta zuba miki bura uba, kin zauna kina aikin wahala kan namiji to idan bake kadan bace sai meye akayi."

"Ai dayake kinga ke kin kafa kin tsare a wajen dad dole ni kice mini haka, daman na dade da sani cewa Dad ne kawai ke sona ke burinki kici mutuncin mahir a gabana ki rabani da shi."

Tayi maganar cikin fushi kai ka rantse da mate dinta take mgn, baki mummy ta rike tace.

"nafisa ni kike fadawa haka?"

Banza tayi da ita tana turo baki ta nufi katuwar wardrobe dinta dake shake da kaya.

Kwafa mummy tayi ta fice daga dakin tana fadin.

"zakici ubanki indai nice dani kike zancen."

Kasa kasa Fina tace.

"badai ubana ba.😓wa iyazubillah yh rabb ka shiryamu karabamu da sharrin zamani ka shirya mana zuri'a. "

Wata 'yar 6ingilar vest marar nauyi tasa iyakar cinyarta ko bra bata saba tasa pant kawai, ta kwanta saman sofa tare da janyo wayarta ta fara kiran layin mahir, zumbur ta Mike zaune tare da dafe kirji ganin line busy tun dazu take kiransa call waiting ynz kuma busy. 

Jikinta rawa kawai yake ta dubi hadadden agogon dakin taga 9 na dare. 

"Kam bura....dawa yake waya this time? "

Da sauri ta danna wayar Samuel bugu daya ya dauka ta hau kansa da ashar. 

"Kai dan abakazan ubanka da uwarka aikin me nasaka ne jiya? Oh kamayar dani yar iska ko wato kudin banza nake ba? No naci uwarka tashanci da rashin mutunci kasan abinda zan iya aikatawa matukar bakayi mini abinda na saka dai dai ba."

Daga chan 6angaren Samuel cikin hausan shi dabata fita sosai yace. 

"ma'am tun dazu na turo miki duk wani information na yarinyar hadda pic din ta."

"ta uwaka ka turon a ina ka turon look Samuel kasan Banson rainin wayo ko?"

"ma'am ki duba whats'app din ki."

Tsaki tayi ta katse wayar tayi saurin shiga what's app din ta, da sauri ta tafi nmbr Samuel gabanta ne ya yanke ya fadin lokacin da taci Karo da lustrous skin din hibba Fuskarta dauke da kyakkyawan murmushi tana sanye da dogon hijjab blue sai likaf a fuskarta amma ta dage shi sama, jikin Fina yayi mugun saki dan ita tunda ma take bata ta6aganin good girl kmr hibba ba, ita kallon sani ma take mata, duk da sanyin dakin amma gaba daya ta jike sharaf, rubutun sama ta fara karantawa komai na tarihin hibba rubuce. 

Sai lokacin ta tuna gurin da ta santa time din da suka hadu zata shiga office din mahir.

Wani murmushin takaici tayi kafin ta dauki daya wayarta ta danna wata number cikin wani irin yare kamar nufanci tace.

"aiki nakeson sakaku."

Shiru tayi kafin ta kuma cewa.

"ah ah dauke mini ita nakeso ayi ku kullen ita tare da gana mata azaba mai radaddi kafin na baku umarnin ku kasheta."

Shiru ta kumayi tana sauraran abinda ake fada mata sannan tace.

"zanyi maka transfer kudi ishashshe, aikin ku ze fara gobe, ynz zan turo ma da duk wani information na yarinyar bana son a sami matsala."

Gyada kai kawai tayi kafin ta ajiye wayar tana sake kallon fuskar hibba sosai, sae ta tsinci kanta Tana hawaye girgiza kai tayi tare da share hawayenta a fili tace.

"sai kin dandani azaba da radaddin da kikasa zuciyata a yau wllh sai kinyi dana sanin sanin mijina da kikayi."

Daga haka ta ajiye wayar tare da nufar fridge ta bude ta dauko wata karamar kwalbar cyrup ta bude ta kwankwade du...hegia kawar Ashe tana dan kora ruwa🤣🤣






Hibba kuwa karfe goma suka gama waya da mahir bayan ya turo mata zazzafan message da kullum sae anyi kusan ba adadi ajiye wayar tayi saman kirjinta tana sake jin wani sabon sonsa na musamman. 

Unty ramla ce ta kirata ta daga da sauri tana fadin.

"yar halak ynz nake zancenki a zuciyata zan kiraki."

Daga 6angaren unty ramla tace.

"ke bana son gulma kina zancen sa dea tun yaushe nake kiranki busy kingama soyayyar zaki ishen da gulma ynz ni kuma."

Dariya hibba ta tuntsire da ita tace.

"kinji ki unty da wani zance kawai dan kinji ina waya sai kice soyayya nake."

"dan Allah Rufan baki kowa yaji muryanki yasan kina cikin farinciki sosai, magana zamuyi gobe kizo gida ina son ganinki."

"ko baki fada ba unty ramla dannima cike nake da son ganinki akwai muhimman maganganun da zamu tattauna, amma ki kira baba ki fada masa dan kinsan baze barni nazo ba."

"na kirashi tun dazu ma,sai Kinzo din plz ki tsaya ta kasuwa ki tahon da zo6o da kayan kamshi."

"okay tohm, ina su amna kawa sunyi barci ko?"

"tun dazu mana nima kwanciya zanyi good night."

Sallama sukayi, ta mike ta fito tsakar gida a bakin famfo tayi alwala sannan ta dawo dakinta ta tada sallah, ta jima tana nafila tare da karatun Alkur'ani mai girma sujjadarta ta karshe ta dade tana addu'ar neman za6in Allah alamuranta tsakanin mahir da anwar duk wanda yake alkhair Allah ya bata shi wanda ba alkhairi ba kuma Allah ya cire masa sonta tare da bashi wadda ta fita domin sosai takejin mugun tausayin anwar ko dan lalurarsa domin tsakanin shi da mahir ta rasa wanda yafi wani sonta, ba ita ta kwanta ba sae da tayi sallahr asuba.

Haka ma 6angaren mahir shima kwana yayi yana sallah domin shi yama riga da ya saba baifiya yin barci da daddare sosai ba, shi isa duk wani tsafin fina da mahaifiyarta befiye kamashi sosai ba.




Washe gari.......
