*🅿 16*

Karfe goma sha daya na safe ta gama shiryawarta tsaf zata tafi gidan unty ramla bakar abaya ta saka tare da mayafinta, dakin baba ta shiga da sallama da yake yau bai fita ba, zaune ta iskeshi kan kujera yana duba wani littafin Islam har kasa ta tsugunna kanta a kasa tace.

"baba na shirya zan tafi."

Dago kansa yayi ya kalleta yana zare medical glass din fuskarta, tsura mata ido yayi Allah ya sani yana son hibba amma baze sakar mata ba matukar bata fitar da miji ba dan ba yarda za'ayi ace yarinya shekara 26 amma tana zaune godegode babu aure, a dan murtuke yace. 

"tace zaki dan kwanar mata biyu koh? "

"ah ah baba ba kwana zanyiba domin gobe zanyi resuming aiki."

Wani kallo yayi mata sannan yace. 

"aikin kenan aiki aiki kullum kamar wata inji, hibbatullah kin san dai ni ba sa'anki bane ba koh?"

Ya tambayeta seriousness.

Kanta a kasa ta gyada masa kai, jinjina kai yayi kafin yace. 

"toh magana ta karshe da zamuyi dake yau akan aure kinji na rantse wallahi tallahi na baki nan da wata guda ki fitar da miji idan ba haka ba kuma, hmmm tashi kije Allah ya kiyaye."

Kai ta kada tare da mikewa tana kokarin mayar da hawayenta. 

Tana fitowa taga umma tsaye jikin bangon dakin tausayin hibha ya sake kama umma, ta share hawayen da suka zubo mata tare da riko hannun ta suka shige cikin uwar dakin umma.

Cike da tausasawa umma tace.

"ki cigaba Hibbatullah har kullum Allah yana tare da masu hakuri, komai da lokacine kema lokacin ki yana nan tafe insha Allahu."

"umma ada baba ba haka yake mini ba amma yanzu duk yabi....."

"ya isa hibba mahaifinkine yafi kowa sonki duk abinda yake miki soyayyace ta jawo haka karki kuskura shedan ya darsa miki wani abu a zuciyarki game da mahaifinki, ki share hawayenki ki tashi ki tafi rana tana kuma kwallewa."

Ba musu hibba ta Mike a sanyaye ta nufi dakinta hand bag dinta ta dauko sai wayarta da flat shoe ta fito, a tsakar gida ta sami umma tana kokarin shiga kitchen tace.

"umma na tafi."

"toh hibba ubangiji Allah ya tsare sai kindawo ki gaishesu."

"toh zasuji. "

Ta fada tana ficewa, a kofar gida suka hadu da rayyan dawowarsa daga sch kenan tace.

"engineer da kansa har an dawo."

"yp ina zaki da tsakar ranar nan?"

"gidan unty ramla."

"tab gsky unty hibba bakyajin rana amma dae ba kwana zakiyi ba."

"kwana zanyi mana."

ta fada cike da tsokana, ido ya zaro tare da dan 6ata face yace. 

"gaskiya ah ah kije dai ki dawo banda kwana."

murmushi tayi tare dajan sumar kansa tace.

"kaga wannan munafukar sumar da ka tara sai an cireta dana dawo insha Allah da kaina zan kaika a cire."

dariya yayi tare da danyi gaba yace. 

"kinga muje na sama miki napep."

Tare suka jero har tahau napep yana daga mata hannu, sae da ta fara biyawa kasuwa ta suyo mata sautinta sannan ta dan siyawa su amna abubuwa ta shige.



Ko da taje gidan bata sami unty ramla ba sai mai aikinta dake zaune kan kujera tana bawa Amna kamu.

Cike da murna da gwaranci Amna ta taho gurinta, ita kuwa ta cha6eta tare da dagata sama tana shillawa tana dariya tace.

"my kawa I missed you too, where's mum?"

Dariya Amna ta 6ingile da ita, hibba ta kalli Kamila da itama murna tasa bakinta kin rufuwa tace.

"unty hibba tun safe nake zuba idon ganinki sannu da zuwa, yah jiki?"

Zama tayi tana daura Amna kan cinyarta tace.

"jiki yayi sauki Kamila badai har ynz untyn bata dawoba?"

"eh ynz zaki ganta dan tace bazata dade ba yau."

"lallaima tun dazu take damuna da yawa, su boy ana sch."

"eh kullum sai sunyi kuka su gurin unty hibba zasu, amma dai kwana zakiyi koh unty?"

"kwana Kamila ah ah tafiya zanyi."

"Kai unty hibba wllh hatta baban boy yace yayi kewar motsinki."

"ke rabani da wadannan iyayen gidan naki."

Ta fada tana karasa bawa Amna kamunta, wayarta dake jaka tayi kara number Anwar ta gani murmushi ta danyi sannan ta daga tare da gaidashi tace.

"yah anwar lafiya naji muryarka so weak."

Wani numfashi ya ja da kyar kafin yace. 

"baby am so sick, tun jiya ina hospital ma now."

ido ta zaro gabanta ya yanke ya fadi tace.

"innalillahi wa'inna ilaihir'raji'un abin har da asibiti amma dai ba ciwon bane ba koh?"

Dan tari yayi na mararsa lafiya yace.

"shine baby."

muryarta ta Fara rawa hawaye suka Fara zubo mata cike da tsananin tausayinsa tace.

"yah rabb ya akayi haka yah anwar kana sa wani abu aranka ko plz mene cikin ranka."

Tari ya farayi da karfi yana salati tare da fadin ta yafe masa ze mutu, Amna ta ajiye ta Mike da sauri ta shige dakin su boy tana sakin kuka sosai. 

"kabar fadin haka yah anwar bazaka mutu ka barmu ba muna sanka ni da Mamie da granny bazamu jure rashinka ba, dan Allah ka daina fadin haka."

Ta fada cikin matsanancin kuka.

Sauke numfashi yake a hnkl yace. 

"baby do you love me? "

cikin matsanancin Kuka tace.

"eh wllh I love you with all my heart plz don't leave me bazan iya jurewa ba plzzzzz....."

Wani tsalle anwar ya daka wanda har ita sai da tajishi yace. 

"yesooo finally she's love me, alhmdllh abinda nakeson ji kenan baby thank you."

wayar ta dago ta kalla da kyau baki sake, domin ita idan Zaka samata wuka a ma'ogwaro batasan abinda ta fada ba, mayar da wayar tayi kunnenta tace.

"yh anwar are you OK? "

"yes cwthart daman kalau nake planing ne kawai, dan ban ta6ajin kince kina sona ba, tabbas nayi dace kuma nagodaiwa Allah Ashe haka kikesona da yawa thanks much, baby bazan ta6arinki ba duk tsanani hibbatullah duk lokacin da rasaki walahi tallah zuciyata Zata karasa bugawa zan iya jure komai a rayuwa amma banda rashinki, na rokeki da girman Allah karki rabu dani ki taimakawa mayan da ya rayu cikin kunci na rashin makusanci ki taimaka ki ceci zuciyar dake aiki da na'ura saboda tsabagen rauninta."

jikin hibba ya gama Saki In banda hawaye babu abinda take tabbas yau anwar ya kulleta da jijiyoyinta ta shiga uku ina zata saka ranta.

"baby kinyi shiru yi hakuri nasa beautiful eyes naki zubar da hawaye plz am sorry ban karawa."

"amma gsky ka daina irin wannan wasan"

"na daina baby indai kullum zaki dinga sanar dani kina sona."

"hmmm."

"okay kina ina?"

"gidan unty ramla."

"nashiga tara ni salihu jikan salihu ynz haka kika fita ke daya kika hau napep, nasan duk mazan duniya Sun gama gane mini mata."

"kajika wai duk mazan duniya, ni bawanda ya ganni."

"ban yarda ba, gsky ni banji dadin fitarki ba tare dani ba, dole idan na dawo nasan abinyi."

"hmm, kaga ni duk ka rudani bari naje nabar yarinya parlour."

dariya yayi tare da manna mata kiss ta kashe wayar da sauri tana dariya kasa kasa, toilet tashiga ta wanko fuskarta sannan ta fito parlour unty ramla tana zaune rungume da Amna dake tsala ihu, Kamila kuma tana zaune. 

Harara unty ramla ta maka mata, ita kuma tayi murmushi ta zauna kusa da ita tare da daukar Amna tana jijjigata tace.

"nashiga uku ni hibba wa yasa mini kawa kuka Kamila? "

"munafuka bake kika samini ita kuka ba tun daga gate nakejin ihun yarinyar nan, wait wai kukan ubanwama kikayi ke?"

Dariya ta bushe da ita ta kalli Kamila tace.

"kuka kuma Kamila wace tayi kuka?"

Kamila zatayi mgn unty ramla tace.

"karkice komai Kamila bar yar rainin sense saurayintane yake kokarinmutuwa shine take kuka munafuka."

Dariya hibba ta sake bushewa da ita tana kissing Amna da tayi shiru tana lumshe ido alamar bacci zatayi.


bayan Sun gama lunch suna zaune parlour suna hira, su biyu nan unty ramla ta nutsu sosai ta fuskanci hibba tace. 

"yar uwa mene tsakaninki da Barr anwa?"

hibba bata da abokiyar shawara sama da yar uwarta basa 6oyewa junansu komai, ta gyara zama da kyau sannan ta fara kwararo mata bayani game da tarihin anwar da soyayyar da yake mata daga nan kuma ta daura da bata labarin Dr mahir da son da take masa tun lokacin da aka kawoshi asibitinsu a matsayin CMD ta dage sosai tana fada mata irin yarda take sonshi fiye da komai ta kuma sanar da ita tausayin anwar da kullum ke sake mamaye ranta.

Ajiyar zuciya unty ramla ta saki tare da cire tagumin da tayi tace.

"tabbas yar uwa kina cikin tsaka me wuya, kina bukatar addu'a sosai tare da neman za6in Allah, amma ke tsakaninki da Allah a cikinsu wa kike ganin zaki iya aure?"

kallon unty ramlan tayi da rinannun idanunta tace.

"inason mahir gsky san da baze misaltuba, amma anwar jigone a taredani zan iya sadaukar D soyayyar mahir ga anwar ko dan ceto raunanniyar zuciyarsa, amma shima mahir din ina....."

"ga shawara hibba ki rabu da wani mahir bawai dan wani abuba wllhi zanfi kowa farinciki idan akace kin sami miji kamar Dr mahir domin mahir namijine mai nagarta da kyawawan halayen da duk wata mace zatayi farincikin samun miji kamarsa, a dan sanin da nayiwa Dr mahir tare da abban boy na gane cewa mahir yana daga cikin waliyyan maza wadanda bamu dasu a wannan zamanin sai dai abu guda matarsa, nasan kinsan wace Dr Nafisa dan baiwa, kwata kwata bata da mutunci bata da imani akan mijinta, hibba babu macen da zata iya zaman kishi da Fina domin bata da tausayi zata iya komai idan nace miki komai to komai da kika sani rashin imani akan mijinta Tana da kudi suna da milki kinga kuwa zata aikata duk abinda taso, bazanso ki jefa kanki cikin hatsariba ke kadece yar uwata mace bazan so ganin rayuwarki cikin hadari ba, yanzu ma rayuwarki tana cikin hatsarine matukar tasan abinda ke tsakaninku. 
Kinga kuwa anwar gashi saurayi bashi da wani aibu gwanda gurin da hnkln ze kwanta."

Numfashi hibba ta sauke tabbas magan ganun untyn ta sunshigeta duk abinda ta fada babu karya a ciki.

"hakane unty amma ya zanyi da Dr kuma yaune ranar dazezo amsar answer soyayyarwa, wllh unty idan na rasashi zan mutu shima yana sona unty ina tausayin anwar zuciyarsa da injina take amfani."

Wannan gwagwatsula ta hibba da yawa take unty ramla ta fada a zuciyarta, kukan da hibba tasa yasa tajanyota jikinta tare da rungumeta tanashafa bayanta cike da tausayawa. 

"kiyi hakuri kibar kukan haka kiyi addu'a ki mikawa Allah za6inki duk wanda Allah yayi mijinkine to ze bawa daya dagana da hakuri."

haka dai 'yar uwa ta gari da kowa yake bidar samu tayi ta kwantarwa da hibba hnkl da kalamai masu sanyi tana sake tunatar da ita saka ubangiji aduk wani lamarinka da bar masa za6i, sun shafe kusan awa 3 suna tattaunawa.

Kamila ce tashigo parlourn da sallama Amna sa6e a kafadarta tace.

"unty gata har tashi tana kukan unty ibanta(hibba)"

dariya hibba tayi tare da amsarta tanafadin.

"kawa har an gyangyada an tashi."

Unty ramla ta dubi agogo ta mike daga shingidar da tayi tace.

"na mutu ni ramlatu, kinga har 4 tayi wai."

"ehmna ae ynz lokaci gudu yake sosai."

"Allah kasa mu cika da imani,bari na Mike kar mijina ya dawo ya ganni haka shirim babu kyan gani."

Dariya hibba tayi tace.

"wai shirim toh Allah na tuba ae ynz kun zama daya."

"wane daya😳hibba da namijin ake zama daya tab irin na da kenan banda mazan ynz dan wllh mazan zamanin nan kyar suke kana watsa ruwan ma ya kuka Kare bare ka zauna haka, bari kiji hajiya kawai ki dage ki daura damar zama karuwar mijinki, dan yasin duk macen da tace zama zatayi sun zama daya da miji hhhh zataga zama kuwa."

"haha haha kina ruwa sister amma a ganina yara uku ke da yah Khalifa ae an zama daya wane dare ne jemage be gani ba in banda na mutuwarsa."

"ae daman ba'a fatan yaga wannan daren hajiya tab, ke kullum fa fil nake zama wajen mijina domin bana wasa da kula da gyara tsaftar jiki gida da yarana, ina kiyaye dokokin hancin mijina da idanuwansa, ke yau ma in fada miki jijji6i nasa unty fati ta dafo mini yau special night zamuyi.."

Hibba sbd da dariya har da hawaye tana dariya tace.

"yauwa Allah zan mutu dariya mene kuma jijji6i unty."

"hehehe au bakisan jijji6iba to ki bari idan kinsha daga ciki zaki sanshi."

"fadan ynz mana ni daina ta6a jin nurses din wajen mu suna mgn sa so bansan mene ba."

Wani dan karamin flacks ta janyo ta bude mata tace kinga jijji6i toh."

"wow nama ne ae."

Ta fada tana kai hannu da sauri ta janye flacks din tana 6oyewa a bayanta tana dariya tace.

"kuji mini kwadayayyiyar 'ya daga nuna miki abu sai ci."

"yaban sha'awane sai kamshi yake wllh yanka daya zan dauka."

"gindin saniyar zakici."

ido hibba ta zare tare da yamutsa fuska tace.

"dan Allah mene?"

"wllh gindin saniyane ake dafawa da magunguna, humm hajia a kalla duk wata ki cishi wato gaban mace ne ke ciccikowa sosai ya matse 6am ta yarda wagga aba🍌sai tayi da gasken gaske zata shiga ga uwa uban ruwan ni'ima kamar famfo, ynz idan na baki wannan ae sae ki kama Dr kiyi masa fyade."

ta fada tana dariya ta Mike ta shige extra parlournta, hibba kuwa ME take IN banda dariya wayartace tayi kara alamar shigar message tana dubawa taga mahir ne ya rubata kamar haka.


_"on my way........ I love you"_

*🅿 17*

Bayan sallahr isha hibba ta fito zata tafi gida babu yarda basuyi da ita ta kwanaba amma taki, har bakin titi unty ramla ta rakota, suna tsaye ta samu dan sahu tashiga. 

Suna cikin tafiya taga sun kauce hanya ta dubi mutumin tace.

"bawan Allah ina zamu kuma?"

"ina kikace zaki?"

"goron dutse mana naga Ka sauka daga hanya."

"sai ki zuba ido ki gani yanke nayi saboda d'ayar hanyar akwai go slow da yawa."

"amma kuwa ka dauko mu da nisa."

Ta fada tana danna Nokia hannunta. 

Wata hanya mai duhu suka shiga nan gaban hibba ya soma faduwa tace.

"wai dan Allah ina zaka kaini ne? Saukeni inkasan bazakabi dani ta hanyar da bansani ba."

"amma ke kuwa ko uwar wutar cikin tsiya gareki ni banson mita kin fini sanin abinda nake ne."

Karfen ta rike cikin tashin hankali tace.

"wllh sai ka saukeni bazan shiga wancan bakikkirin din duhun can ba."

Fuzgar dai daita sahun yayi da karfi,  tare da jan wani wawan burki Wanda yasa hibba dakuwa da karfen napep din, rike kanta tayi tare da furta "yah salam"

Wata katuwar jeep ce tsaye gabansu,  wasu gidiga gidigan maza suka fito su biyar daga motar duk da cikin duhu ne amma kallo daya zakayiwa fuskarsu kasan babu digon imani a tare dasu, da sauri hibba taja da bawa tana kiran.

"innalillahi wa'inna ilaihir'raji'un, allahuma ajirni fil musiba wakhlifni khairan min..... "

Bata kai karsheba daya daga cikinsu ya fuzgota da karfi tare da shaka mata handkerchief a hanci nan take ta fadi kasa a sume, bandir din kudi yan dubu dubu daya daga cikinsu ya bawa Mai napep din ya kar6a jikinsa na tsuma domin shi kansa tsorace yake da wadannan mutanen,  hanya suka saita masa sukace ya 6ace daga nan ai kuwa da gudu mai napep din ya arta baya ko juyowa, cikin mota suka zura hibba tare da tattaro Jakarta da wayarta suka zura motar sukayi gaba.



Su umma na zaune tsakar gida suna sauraron radio karfe 9:15 rayyan yana wani assignment a wayarsa ya dago tare da kallon umma dake jingine da pillow tun dazu bata iya ko magana yace.

"umma kinga unty hibba me cewa bazata kwana ba har ynz daman nasan ta fada ne kawai amma gidan unty ramlar zata ta dawo a yini daya suda idan sukaga hadu kamar zasu hadiye juna."

Umma ta sauke ajiyar zuciya domin ita tun magriba gabanta ke faduwa tace.

"kirata a waya muji idan kwanan zatayi amma ni nan bata ce mini zata kwana ba."

Baba dake rike da radio ya ajiye sannan yace.

"A kirata kice mata me? Kema kinsan tunda takai I yanzu bazata dawoba suna can suna aikin dariyar su."

Dariya rayyan yayi yace.

"wllh kam dan idan akace Sun hadu musamman da su unty zarah(matar yah mudan) da unty safna(yah abbakar)  yayyen hibba ae abun ba'a magana."

Dariya sukayi banda umma da tayi shiru ita dai ji take ajikinta akwai wani abu a kasa.



6angaren Mahir ma ya gama shirin kwanciyarsa tsaf ya kwanta a lafiyayyen gadonsa duk da yana cikin farinciki samun soyayyar hibba amma yanayin jikinsa yana nuna masa cewa something is wrong.

Wayarsa ya janyo tare da danna mata kira, sai dai har takatse ba'a daga ba sake kira yayi ba'a d'agawa sai da yayi mata wajen 30 missed call amma bata dagawa daga karshe ma sai ta dinga fadin is not reachable.

Mikewa yayi zaune tare da fadin.

"what's wrong with her ne? Bata picking call yah rabb yasa everything alhmdllh. "

Dafe gefan kirjinsa yayi yanajin yarda gabansa ke tsananta faduwa zuciyarsa na wani fat fat da sauri, still sake kiran wayar yayi bata shiga mikewa yayi ya shiga bathroom tare da dauro alwala yazo yasa jallabiya tare da tada sallah. 



Haka takema ta 6angaren Anwar babu irin kalar kiran dabeyi mataba amma bai samunta, gaba daya yagama shiga damuwa da tashin hankali yanason kiran wayar umma amma yanajin nauyi, dakyar ya samu bacci ya daukesa tare da bakaken mafarkai mararsa kyau wadanda baisan nameye ba shi dai abinda yasan ze iya tunawa shine kukan da hibba keyi mai tsanani tana kiran sunan wani baya gane me take fadi amma bashi take kiraba, da ciwon kai ya tashi yayi sallahr asuba a wahalce tare da shirin dawowa kano.





6angaren su hibba kuwa da wadanda suka kamata tafiya sukayi mai tsananin tsayisun ketare daji daji da dama, basu suka isa wani katon daji mai tsananin girma da tsayiba sai karfe bakwai na safe duk kuwa da gudun da suka dinga shararawa wani gida mai tsayin gaske suka nufa ginin tsaf irin na prison, a bakin gidan sukayi parking tare da fiffitowa sai lkcn na Kare musu kallo da kyau kaf dinsu ba hausawa bane irin nufawa din nan ne masu munin halitta fuskarsu duk dinki ansha yanka.

Daya daga cikinsu wanda daga gani kasan shine babba ya dubi yaran nasa cikin yaran nufanci yace. 

"kudauketa ku shigar da ita ciki."

Mutun daya ne ya sa6i hibba dake barci kamar gawa ya shigar da ita cikin gidan gaba daya Kansan gidan takin awakaine da ciyawa, wani daki mai duhun gaske suka jefata wanda shima takin ne kawai aciki da ciyawa ya fito.

Ogan yana shan sigari ya danna wayarsa tare da karawa a kunnensa. 

Bugu uku aka katse kiran wayar tare da kira.

"ma'am komai ya kammala mun iso."

Daga daya 6angaren akace.

"inaso ku hanata ci da sha na tsawon kwana uku."

"angama ma'am."

Kit aka datse kiran, kuma duk maganar da sukayi sunyitane cikin yaran nufanci.


Karfe 9 dai dai hibba ta fara motsa kafarta a hankali idanunta da sukayi mata azabar nauyi ta fara budewa a hankali har tagama budesu, a hnkl ta motsa hannunta sai kuma ta Mike da sauri tana waige waige tsananin duhun dakin dako tafikan hannunka baka gani ta farabi da kallo, tsaye ta mike jikinta na wani irin rawa, inbada zare ido babu abinda takeyi, abinda ya faru da ita ta fara tunawa, wata kara ta saki tare da daura hannuwanta duka biyun kanta tana fadin.

"innalillahi wa'inna ilaihir'raji'un nashiga uku na ummata babana ina ne nan?"

ta karasa maganar tana Saki wani irin  kuka, Mikewa Tayi da sauri wane mahaukaciya ta fara laluban Jakarta tana neman wayarta jakar kawai ta gani ta zazzageta a kasa sai dai babu wayarta a ciki, kuka take kamar ranta ze fita tana fadin ina ne nan sai dai babu amsa😓



har washe gari Mahir na kiran wayar hibba zuwa ynz a rufe take, gaba daya baya cikin nutsuwarsa gashi duk karatun alkur'anin da yayi gabansa bai bar dancing ba, lokaci guda wani zazzafan zazzabi ya rufeshi na fargabar mai yasa wayanta baya shiga, yana kwance cikin blanket kiran fina ya shigo wayarsa sai dai har ta katse bai dagaba haka ta kira har so uku amma baya dagawa. 

Daga 6angaren Fina kuwa baki ta dan ta6e sai kuma ta tuntsire da dariya afini tace.

"kayi kagama."

Mum da dad suka kalleta sai ta dan yamutsa fuska ta Mike tana danna kiran telephone din gidan. 

Kuku dake shirya breakfast a dinning ne yayi picking cikin girmamawa ze fara gaidata a dakile ta dakatar dashi cike da gadara da izza tace.

"Dr yafita ne."

"nop hajiya I tot bai fito bama."

Mtsw." taja tsaki tare da katse kiran.



2:00pm

Umma ta dubi rayyan tace.

"kai auta kiramin hibba a waya nagaji da wannan shirun."

"umma tun dazu nake kiran wayarta ta a kashe wllh itama dai unty hibba ta fidda kudi tasiyi waya taki ta tsaya da Nokia."

"kai banson magana kiran ramlatu toh."

Wayarsa ya cira daga charge ya kamo number unty ramla tashiga ta datse kiran sai gashi ta kira tana dagawa yace.

"unty ramla kirafa nayi kikayi wani rejrctive."

unty ramla tace.

"enyeh auta kenan yan university an fara samun ribar kudin transport kenan."

Dariya yayi sannan yace.

"to mugaisa ina wuni yah su boy da jidda da mahaifiya."

"duk muna lfy lao kana gida tun jiya nake neman wayar hibba da umma bata shiga baba ma nakirashi dazu bayajina bani hibba dan Allah."

Daga umma har rayyan shocked suka shiga, network din kansu dif ya dauke, unty ramla tace.

"Hello rayyan kanajina."

Ina babakin mgn umma tayi ta maza ta amshi wayar cikin rawar jiki dukkan wani part najikinta Rawa yake tace.

"ramlatu mai kike cewa daman hibba ba a gidanki ta kwanaba ko da safe ta taho."

Unty ramla taji tmbyr bankwarakwai tace.

"umma me kike fadane ai tunjiya ta taho dan babu yarda abban boy beyi da itaba akan ta kwana amma taki dakaina muka rakota har cikin dan sahu mai kike cewa ne."

"lahaula fishi'atullah innalillahi wa'inna ilaihir'raji'un subhanallah."

shi kawai umma ke maimaitawa wayar ta su6uce a hannunta ta fadi, rayyan kuwa gaba daya battery din kansa yayi shutdown, unty ramla itama da gaba daya ta gama gigicewa dakyar ta iya kiran Khalifa ta sanar dashi halin dake faruwa, daga ita sai riga da wando ta fito, a bakin layin suka hade da Khalifa dakyar ya ta yarda suka koma gida ta chanza kaya tasa hijjab suka fito, su yah mudan da abbakar har da babban yayansu dake kaduna sai da aka kira ko hibba tazo amma ah ah. 

Karfe hudu gaba daya family din yaya da jikoki da surukai sun hallara a gidan, tsayawa fadin tashin hankalin da gidan ke ciki 6ata lkc ne domin hannu baze iya rubutawa ba kawai ku hararo tsananin tashin hnkln da mutun ze shiga idan karasa wani naka ta hanyar 6acewa.

Duk iyakar cigiya gidan yan uwa da kawaye babu inda ba'ajeba, umma itadai tana zaune shirim tsakar gida bata iya motsa koda dan yatsanta jikokin gidan kuwa kowa kuka yake duk taurin zuciya irin ta baba idan ya kalli fuskar zuri'artasa sai yayi hawaye lokaci guda 6atan hibba ya karade unguwa gaba daya unguwar ta zama kamar anyi mutuwa kowa jimami. 


Jikin mahir yayi tsanani da yawa amma yarda yakeji baze hanashi zuwa yaga halin da hibbarsa take ciki ba dakyar ya Mike rike da bango yayo wanka jallabiya kawai ya zura sai wayarsa da carkey dinsa ya fito kafarsa sanye da Gucci slippers, driver yabawa key domin shi baze iya driving ba suka nufi gidan su hibba yana kwance a back seat jikinsa in banda rawa babu abinda yake.

Shima Anwar hankali tashe ya nufo gidan su hibbar shi da granny domin rayyan ya kirashi ko yasan inda hibba take,  hnkl tashe suka nufo gidan su hibbar kusan a tare motar mahir da Anwar ta tsaya, a gigice Anwar da granny suka fito suka nufi cikin gidan. 

mahir kuwa yayi kir da ido yanabin bawan Anwar da kallo tabbas shine wanda suka hadu dashi da ita a green pack.

Jiki ba kwari ya fito daga motar kafarsa har hardewa take tsabar rawa da jikinsa yake, yazo shiga cikin gidan ya dan tafi luu ze fadi driver yayi saurin rikoshi sai yayi saurin dafe bango kansa ya dan dafe sannan ya Mike yashiga a zaure ya tsaya ya umarci driver yayi sallama. 


yah mudan ne ya leko, kir yayi yana kallon mahir dake rike da kansa tabbas kamar ya sanshi amma ya rasa inda ya sanshi. 

"bismillah ku shigo."

Dakyar ya daga kafarsa yabi bayansa cikin gidan, dim dim gabansa ya fadi jin maganar baba yana sanar dasu anwar abinda ya faru.

Dai dai ya karasa shigowa cikin tsakar gidan, tsaye dukkansu suka Mike no wonder professional doctor Mahir mijin yar shugaban kasa doctor Fina ne.

Meya kawoshi gidan kuma cikin wannan yanayin, wani irin jirine yadebeshi ai kuwa ya fadi luuuuuuuu a sume.........

*🅿 19*

Kansa sukayi cikin sauri driver ya tallafoshi ya fado jikinsa, ruwa aka samo aka yayyafa masa, bayan wasu 'yan sakanni ya sauke nannauyar ajiyar zuciya kafin ya fara motsa bakinsa a hankula idanuwansa a rufe, Khalifa Abban boy yace. 

"Dr open your eyes plz."

"hibbahhh!"

Shine abinda ya furta a hankula, gaba d'aya gurin kowa shiru yayi yana mamakin hadi hibba da handsome man like mahir.

Kamar an fuzgeshi ya Mike tsaye jikinsa na wani irin rawa idanuwansa sunyi masifar ja jijiyoyin kansa duk sun mimmike rud'u rud'u. 

Gaban baba ya nufa tare da zuba gwiwoyinsa kansa, cikin raunin murya yace. 

"baba where's my love,  tell me jiya muna tare hibba tace tana sona zata aureni baba tana ina.... "

Wani irin fizga Anwar yayiwa mahir yana wani irin huci kamar mayunwacin zaki, shakeshi yayi da bango cikin daga murya yace. 

"ubanwane ya baka ita who are you da Zaka dinga dangana matata da abar sonka, I said who are?"

Ya karasa mgnr cikin daga murya yana wani irin jijjigashi,  wani irin turi yayi masa tare da zuba masa wasu kyawawan maruka masu kyau, ya daga hannu ze sake marinsa granny tayi saurin rike hannunsa ranta a matukar 6ace.

Ta daukeshi da wani irin mari mai zafin gaske.

"bawanka ne yaron da bashi da cikakkiyar lafiya zaka mara kai ubanwaye."

Hannun mahir dafe da kuncinsa, karo na biyu kenan tun tasowarsa da wata ko wani ya daura hannunsa a kumatunsa da sunan Mari. 

Tabbas matar nan badaban girmanta ba da yau sai ya nuna mata cewa irinta dubu basu isa ba.

Jijjiga kansa yayi cike da takaicin marin yayi wani irin murmushi tare da kallon driver yayi gaba suka fita daga gidan. 

Granny tayi saurin riko anwar tana kuka tace.

"sannu alhj Allah ya saka maka bakin mugu azzalumi bazamu ta6a yafe masa ba."

Gurin dai gaba daya kowa tsit yayi suna cike da mamakin abinda ke faruwa. 



Mahir kuwa suna fita direct CP na jihar Kano ya kira tare da sanar dashi duk halin da ake ciki na 6atan hibbar, hotunanta da ya dinga daukarta jiya uncontable ya tura masa, daga haka suka nufi gidansa jikinsa na tsananta zafi. 

Su baba gaba daya suka tafi police station domin suyi report 6atan hibban sai dea abin mamaki duk police station din da sukaje sai suji cewa sun san da sanarwar abin ba karamin mamaki ya basuba Khalifa ne kawai yasan cewa aikin bana kowa bane sai na Dr mahir.

*🅿 10*

Hasken dakin ne ya sake bayyana wannan yasa hibba saurin mayar da idanuwanta ta rufe sbd wani irin dum da sukayi mata.

Farar kafartace ta bayyana sanye cikin wani uban takalmi mai shegen tsini kafarnan kamar ta jarire, hibba tayi saurin dago da kanta sai dai bata Iya ganin fuskar mata sbd fitilar iyakar ita kade ta haska. 

Feena ce sanye cikin wasu classic English wear fuskar nan a daure babu alamar fara'a a tare da ita, bayanta wadannan da suka dauko hibba ne, kujera suka ajiye mata ta zauna tana wani ya mutsa fuska, fitilar aka kashe sae kuma aka kunna ta gaba daya dakin, idanu hibba ta fara zarewa tanabin dakin da kallo wasu zafaffun kwalla suka zubo mata, ta maida kallonta da feena dake mata wani irin kallo, iyakar tsorata da fargaba hibba tayi da ganin feena.

Feena ta dubi daya daga cikin yaranta,  da sauri ya miko mata wani mug, ta bude tare da mikewa ta nufi hibba tana mata wani kallo hibba faraja da baya tayi tana girgiza mata kai idanuwanta kamar an bude famfon hawaye, batayi aurena taji feena ta watsa mata zazzafan ruwan barkono a fuskata da jikinta, wani irin kara ta saka tare da mikewa tana daka tsalle idanuwanta a rufe.

Wani murmushin mugunta feena tayi kafin tace cikin isa da kasaita. 

"bude idanunki."

Cikin matsanancin kuka hibba tace.

"kiyi mini rai dan girman Allah ki fitar dani daga nan wllh na horu da azabarki nayi miki alkawarin barin rayuwarki da ta mijinki har abada."

Murmushin takaici feena tayi tace.

"good Ashe kinsan laifinki, dan ubanki open ur eyes."

Ta fada a tsawace, hibba girgiza kai tayi tana fadin.

"I can't plz."

"okay. "

Ta fada a mugance alama tayi masu da ido ae kuwa hibba batayi aune ba taji Sun dauketa Sama, ihu ta farayi tana yarfa kafa zuwa ynz muryarta dakyar take Iya fita, wani daki suka shigar da ita wanda dakin gaba daya glass ne, ciki suka shigar da ita tare da daddaure hannayenta a sama, feena ta dafata tace.

"hibba kike or what kinyi gangancin shiga rayuwata bansan bani da imani har hakaba sai danaga kina kokarin rabani da mijina, bari kiji *NI DA DR MAHIR* mutu karaba, so ki ajiye wannan zaki dawwama anan gidan har karshen rayuwarki."

Hibba tayi wani irin murmushi mai cike da tarin takaici cikin dakiyar zuciya tace.

"zaki iya aikata komai a rayuwata kici nasara domin ke marar imani ce, amma abu gudane bazaki iyaba shine raba zuciyar Dr mahir da son hibba ba, domin sona da kaunarsa a cikin jinin jikinsa suke, kuma kiji baki isa kiyi mini abinda Allah beyi mini ba kuma *NI DA DR MAHIR* zamu rayu rayuwa kuwa har ta abada."

Feena da tacika saura kiris ta fashe tayi wata irin dariya tare da dafa kafadarta ta sunkuyo da kanta dai dai fuskar hibbar tace.

"muzuba mugani shege ka fasa."

Daga haka ta hankadata baya, ta fice, tana fita hibba ta fashe da wani irin mahaukacin kuka tana kiran sunan Allah a fili, ruwa ne ya fara zubowa a kanta a hankali a hankali kamar shower lumshe idanuwanta tayi cike da azabar zuciya. 





Mahir ne zaune a office din CP yana sauraron bayanin da yake masa akan irin binciken da mutanensu suke gudanarwa sai dea har ila yau zance daya ne babu wata tartibiyar magana CP ya daura da cewa cikin muryar kwantar da hankali. 

"kayi hakuri Dr insha Allah sae yarda karfin mu ya Kare zamu tsaurara bincike fiye da wanda muke, ku dai kuje ku cigaba da addu'a insha Allah soon zata dawo cikin koshin lfy."

Mahir ya gyada kai tare da sauke ajiyar zuciya yace. 

"but sir I think 6atan hibba ba kidnappers case bane cos da kidnapping akayi da tuni Sun fadi abinda za'a basu zuwa yanzu."

"nima bana tunanin kidnapping nata akayi nafi tunanin hannu ne ya dauketa."

gyada kai yayi yace. 

"I think nima."

"dole zamuje mu gudanar da bincike akan mutanen da take mu'amana dasu."

"okay CP plzz dan Allah ka taimaka kayi mini duk wani kokari."

"karka damu Dr insha Allahu zanyi iyakar iyawata zan taimakeka mahir domin kafi karfin komai a gurina karka manta sanadiyarka da mahaifinka nake wannan matakin da nake, to dan me bazanyi amfani da damata ba wajen farantaka,  Dr calm down very soon her return to you by the God grace."

Dan dadi mahir yaji ya Mike tare da mika masa hannu suka sake musabaha sukayi sallama, har bakin motarsa ya rakoshi kafin sukayi sallama yashiga mota driver yaja. 


wayarsa ya duba yaga miss calls din feena kamar hauka, tsaki yaja tare da danna kiran wayar Mum da yaga miss calls dinta.

Mum cikin sauri ta dauka ta fara magana cikin fada domin batasan abinda ke faruwa ba tace.

"love ka rainani ko?"

"Mum kiyi hakuri."

"keep quiet silly child, kullum inyi hakuri where are you now?"

Mahir relaxing yayi yana shafa kyakkyawar kasumbarshi yace. 

"darling ina Kano but..."

Katse shi tayi da fadin.

"meka fada mini jiya."

"mum cewa nayi zanzo yola today."

"okay yanzu kana yola?"

"mum wallahi...."

"dallah rufen baki nasan maganinka zakaci ubanka."

Daga haka ta datse kiran bata bashi damar fadin abinda ze fada ba, lumshe ido yayi yanajin wata sabuwar damuwa a tare da shi, hanyar gida zasu kama ya umarci driver da su nufi gidan su hibba.



Suna isa kofar gidan anwar na fitowa, sai da naji mugun tausayin anwar dan gayu domin tsakanin shi da mahir ban san wanda yafi wani ramewa ba, wani mugun kallo anwar yayiwa mahir shi kuwa mahir ko inda yake be kalla ba domin batashi yake ba.

Motarsa yashiga yabar wajen ransa a 6ace, shi kuma mahir yayi tsaye yana danna wayarsa, rayyan ne yazo shiga gidan ganin mahir tsaye ya matso inda yake tare da danyi masa murmushi yace. 

"ya ka tsaya daga nan baka shiga ciki ba."

Murmushi mahir Yayi masa tare da mika masa hannu suka gaisa rayyan yace.

"kashigo dan Allah kasan baba yace idan kazo ka dinga shiga."

nagodai yace tare da bin bayansa.....

*🅿 21*

A firgice ya farka daga nannauyan barcin da ya daukeshi tun bayan sallahr asuba wanda rabonsa da yayishi tun kafin 6acewar hibba.

_mafarkin, wani kasurgumin dajine wanda yake cike da mugayen namin daji, hibba ce ke tsala gudu cikin wani irin hali bayanta wasu mutanene ke binta da wasu munanan makamai, sai gudu take tana ihu, daga bayan mutanen kuma anwar ne yana abin mutnen domin ya samu ya ceto hibbar, wani katon rami ta tafi zata fada anwar yayi wata irin kara tare da tsalle yasha gabanta, yana kokarin janyota wani farin haske daga cikin ramin ya fito da gudu hibba tayi gurin wannan haske tana ihu tare da kiran sunan *MAHIR* a hankali wannan haske ya fara washewa fuskar mahir ta bayyana dai dai za'a chakamata wuka yayi saurin tarewa wannan wuka ta sameshi, wani mahaukacin ihu hibba ta Saki tare da fadawa kan mahir itama wukar ta caketa_

Innalillahi wa'inna ilaihir'raji'un shine kawai abnd anwar ke fada domin ya jima yana wannan mafarke mafarken hibba da wannan haske sae yau ya tabbatar da waye wannan hasken, tabbas mahir shine mafaran komai a rayuwar hibba kuma shine hasken rayuwarta, domin mahir yafishi son hibba, wani irin zugi yaji zuciyarsa ta fara yayi sauri rike gefan kirjinsa tare da furta yah salama jini ya fara zuba daga hancinsa da bakinsa dasauri ya fara wani irin tari, kafin kace mai guda gudan jini sun fara zubowa, a daddafe ya mike yayi saurin shiga toilet tare da rufewa ya zura kansa jikin sink yana wani irin tari gwanin ban tausayi😭wayyo anwar dina.

Mum bata sake ce masa komai ba tace yaje yayi wanka yahuta suci abinci ba musu ya Mike ya nufi hadadden part dinsa na gidan.

*🅿 22*

*🅿 23*

Tsaye kawai yayi yanajin dacin zuciyarsa na dade karuwa tare da ayyana hukuncin dazewa feena matukar ya tabbatar da hannunta a 6acewar hibbar sa.

"baby am sorry."

Gira daya ya dauke mata yace.

"unhm for?"

"for.... "

Hannu ya daga mata kafin ya ra6a ta kusa da ita ze wuce yana fadin.

"excuses me."

Da ido feena tabishi tana kallonsa zuciyarta na wani irin suya, da gudu tabar part dinsa ta nufi nata cikin bedroom dinta tashi tana sakin wani irin gujin kuka nan ta fara tarwatsa kayan shafen gaban mirror tana wani irin kuka wane mahaukaci komai Ta gani sae da ta fasa shi, cikin kukan take fadin.

da yake aci uwarta jin kai ko iskarta be kallaba har ta fice ranta na sake 6aci.

Dakin da hibba take tashiga a takure ta ganta tana barci hasken fitila mai haske ya haske dakin gaba daya, ganin yarda hibbar ta koma tayi da kyau kafin ta umarcesu bata ruwa mai sanyi suna kawo mata ta kwara hibba a firgice ta mike tana salati.

"yar uwa kinyi goshi kai alhmdllh wani jinkirin alkhairi ne yar uwa Allah yabaki miji mai kokarin faranta ran matarshi ubangiji Allah ya tabbatar muku da alkhairi hibba kaya alhmdllh akwatuna goma sha biyu."

Wata irin buda kawayen hibba suka saka nan take gida ya sake hautsinewa ita dae hibba gefe taja tana ambaton sunan Allah saboda yarda takejin faduwar gaba.

Sai da mutane suka watse yazamana saura na cikin tsakar gida kawai sannan hibba ita da Rashida suka shiga gidan su unty ramla ne zaune da unty Asiya unty zee da unty zarah sai kanwar Umma da yayansu kawai zaune suna fama gyagygyara kayan, suna jin shigowar hibba aka saki buda tayi saurin shigewa dakin Umma tana rufe fuskarta unty ramla tace.

"munafuka fito kiga abin arziki duk cikinmu waye abokin kunyarki."

Gaba daya suka hada baki da cewa.

"babu suna dariya Rashida Taga kaya sosai tayi murna sallahr isha tayi sannan sukayi sallama ta tafi.

Gidan duk babu kowa sae yaya da jikoki da surukai har yayyunta maza duk suna zaune a tsakar gida baba ma yana zaune babu bakin mgn da ganin kaya unty Ramla da unty Zeey suke budewa su baba kayan suna gani sai da suka gama ganin komai chif kafin baba yayi ajiyar zuciya yace. 

"Allah ya sanya albarka."

Duk sukace amin Umma tace.

"malan kaga kaya ko kaga yarda yaron nan yayi 6arna da kudi?"

Baba yace. 

"tabbas kaya sunyi kuma muma Allah yabamu ikon fitar da ita kunya a wajen su kamar yarda sukayi mata, kuma ae babu laifi matukar kana da iko da hali idan har kayiwa matar da zaka aura wannan yana nuni da cewa yaron meye ne insha Allahu,kuma bakisan wani abuba salamatu ai duk wanda beyi kyashin barin dansa yayiwa yar wasuba to wllh shima baza'ayi ayi kyashin yiwa tasuba, hibba gadai shinan kin gani kinga yarda mijinki ke kaunarki tun baki shiga gidansa ba abunda ya rage miki shine kiyi biyayya ga mijinki yi nayi bari nabari karkiji karkigani ki kyautatawa mijinki gashi nan dai ni da mahaifiyarku shekarunmu 50 ba wani abu da aure amma dai dai da rana daya bata ta6a tafiya gidansu da sunan yaji ba haka zamuyi mubunne kayanmu babu wanda yaji bare ya gani toh haka nakeso daku gaba dayanku ku zauna lfy a gidan auren ku rayuwar dole hakurine acikinsa dan zamane tsakanin jinsi biyu karkudauka shi auren nan kawai aji dadine ah ah dole wataran sae wani ya sa6a dan uwansa, ku zauna lfy agidan auren ku shine farincikin ko wanne iyaye suka yaransu suna zaune lfy gidan mazajensu Allah ubangiji yasanya muku albarka tare da iyalinku Yayi maku albarka."

Kaf tsakar gidan kowa yayi shiru yana jin nasihar baba ba wanda jikinsa beyi sanyi ba, hira suka dan ta6a kafin ko wacce tabi mijinta suka tafi unty ramla ma yah Khalifa yazau ya dauketa ya zamana saura yan kd domin su sai gobe zasu wuce.


Karfe tara mahir ya Kira hibba.......

Mahir ne tare da Anwar zaune akan wasu fararen kujeru dake farfajiyar shop rite gabansu table ne da aka ajiye masu lemo da ruwa, Mahir ya sauke ajiyar zuciya yace.

Ya furta a hankali yana lumshe idanuwansa.

9:15pm

Wayar hibba tadau kara, suhaila da ta fito daga toilet taji ringtone din wayar, daukar wayar tayi sannan ta fito parlour tana kwallawa hibbar kira.

"unty hibba wayarki tundazu ake kira."

Hibba dake zaune tsakiyar yaran gidan homework ta dago tare da amsar wayar number anwar ta gani, baki ta dan turo a fili tace.

"yah anwar ae nayi fushi."

Dariya yaran suka fashe da ita jin yarda tayi mgnr lyk baby girl, ita yar dariyar tayi tana danna kiran nashi rejective call din yayi sannan ya kira mikewa tayi tashige daki tana amsa kiran.

"nayi fushi yah anwar."

Ta fada a shagwa6e, daga 6angaren anwar kuwa lumshe jajayen idanuwasa yayi da yasha kuka dazu sosai, yanajin wata sabuwar kaunarta na ratsa duk wani sako na jikinsa, cikin sanyi murya yace. 

"sorry angel yakike? "

"lafiya yah anwar ya granny nd mamy?"

Shiru ya danyi sannan yace.

"hibba ashe kiyayen da kike mini har takai ta shafi abonkina?"

Ya tambayeta kmr beson mgn. 

Dim gaban hibba ya fadin tayi saurin cewa. 

"yah anwar bangane me kake nufi ba, nifa nakasa gane abinda kake nufi dani."

"mene baki gane a maganata ba hibba ina miki magana ne akan Dr mahir."

ajiyar zuciya mai karfi ta sauke cikin sanyin murya tace.

"yah anwar kayi hak...."

"no hibba karkice komai na san komai kuma alhmdllh nayi muku kyakkyawar fahimta wllh hibba bakomai za6in Allah ne babu yarda zanyi."

Nan dai cikin laushin murya yayi tayiwa hibba bayanin yarda da kaddara tare da tunatar da ita cewa babu wanda ze aure matar da bata shiba sannan ya daura dacewa. 

"yanzu ya za'ayi da abokin nawa wllh yana cin wani hali hibba."

"uhm ni bangane ba."

Ta fada in cool voice.

"kigane mana, ya za'ayi kiyi dumping Dr just saboda matarsa."

ya fada cikin nutsuwa. 

"hmmm yah anwar."

"hibba meye wani hmm yh anwar, angel plz don't do that to Mahir, wllh yana sonki fiye da yarda kike tsammani yana miki son da ko kansa bewa wllh kaunar da yake miki bakya masa kwatan kwacinta."

Hibba dea shiru kawai tayi tana sauraron muryanshi dake fita cike da nutsuwa. 

Anwar ya cigaba da cewa.

"kinga I did not screw you even if I did it should not stop you love for mahir, ko kina tsoron matarshi ne?"

ya fada yana biting lip dinshi.

"ni ban tsoron ta wallahi."

Ta fada kamar zatayi kuka.

"okay, then plz ki amice masu ku dawo kamar yarda kuke da, if it is bcos of me zan bar kasar gaba daya zan koma wajen mamy da aiki kinga I will not visit."

Dasauri hibba tace.

"no yah anwar don't leave me plz."

"okay then?"

Shiru tayi ba tare da tace Komai ba cikin ranta cike da tarin tunanin hali irin na anwar lallai anwar dan aljanna ne domin yana daga cikin bayi masu sowa dan uwansu abinda suke sowa kansu ga uwa uba hakuri da ko wanne irin hukuncin ubangiji. 

Katse mata tunaninta yayi da fadin.

"plz say something karki watsan kasa a ido."

Still shiru hibba tayi tanajin nauyin furtawa anwar din wata kalma akan mahir domin daga jin muryanshi kasan karfin hali kawai yake.

Ajiyar zuciya anwar yayi ya kuma cewa.

"baby plz! Don't do this to us, wllh idan ma ni kikeji wallahil azim har ga rabb nabarmasa ke domin yafini sonki shine alkhairin ki."

Ya fada cikin whisper.

dakyar ta furta.

"I have to think."

"haba dear, dan Allah don't dump mahir, wllh yana sonki fiye da yarda kike tunani, I know you will be a good influence to his life, plz help him and Allah help you too."

Ya fada cikin sanyin murya.

"okay Allah ya za6a mafi alkhairi, but let me istikhara about it."

"better dear, should I call you to know your istikhara."

Dasauri tace 

"no need yah anwar."

Dariya yayi sannan yace.

"hibba kinsan me? "

"ah ah."

"kinsan tsakanin ni da mahir wa yafi cancanta ya moreki? "

Shiru hibba tayi tanason jin abinda ze fada.

Ya daura da cewa.

"duk nafisu cancanta da ke, you know why?  but no problem but since I can't have you don't let my friend loose you, plz I'm begging you."

Ya fada cikin nutsuwa, hibba ita ynz gaba daya mamaki maganganunsa suke bata, what does that mean, ta tmby kanta.

"kinji kanwata."

"tohm."

"yauwa angly angly nerh thank for the time, kina bukatar wani abu?"

"ah ah yh anwar nagodai. "
Ta fada a sanyaye. 

Dariya ya danyi sannan yace.

"kinsan fa yanzu am like a yaya mudan to you, so ki fadan in kina bukatar wani Abu."

Dariya tayi sannan tace.

"bana bukatar komai yh anwar."

"okay don't worry but plz if kinason wani abu ki sanar dani and plz don't dump Dr mahir plz and plz sister."

"insha Allahu yh anwar jazakallahu bil jannah thank you so much."

"Ameen dear wa iyyaki uwc."

Daga haka sukayi sallama ta ajiye wayar zuciyarta fal da tausayin anwar din tare da tarin soyayyar mahir.

Anwar kuwa yana ajiye wayarsa ya cusa kansa cikin pillow yana wani irin kuka mai ban tausayi kiran da mahir yayi masane yasashi sauri daidaita kansa suka gaisa daga 6angaren mahir yace cikin sanyin murya.

"bro I think something is very wrong with you, what happened to you brother."

6oyayyiyar ajiyar zuciya ya sauke yana sake daidaita muryanshi sosai yace. 

"babu komai wllh Dr na fara bacci ne kawai."

"ayyah sorry na tasheka ban sani ba."

"karka damu daman zan kiraka."

Nan ya sheda masa duk yarda sukayi da hibba sosai mahir yayi farinciki ya yi ta zubawa anwar godiya har sae da ya datse kiran.



_A gaggauce.... After 1mounth_

Tsakanin hibba da mahir abin sae son barka da wata irin zazzafar soyayya sukema junansu mai cike da ilimi da tsafta duk Friday yana tafe a kd anan yake weekend dinsa, 6angaren uwargida feena kuwa ynz alhmdllh suna dan mgn da mahir dan har Sun dawo kamar da sai dae sam baya sakar mata kamar a baya.

Iyayen mahir har sunzo sun kawo kudin auren hibba da saka rana inda suka tsayar da wata buyi kachal domin shi baba yace tunda ansan halin yaro no need a tsaya aja biki da nisa, hibba anata shirye shirye sai dai har ynz unty Asiya ta hanata dawowa kano domin gyara na musamman take mata kasancewarta shuwa.

Hamshakiya mandiya uwargida rangida farar Mace alkyabbar mata wane yace bake ba uwargida a gidan Dr mahir🤣Dr feena kenan zaune take a main parlour tana sanye da wata super Holland ja anyi mata dinki straight gown ta kamata sosai irin hannun nan ne Wanda kafada ce kawai ba hannu kanta yasha kananun kalba ta original attachment, tana zaune ta daura kafa daya kan daya tana girgiza su da alama yau ran manyan mata a 6ace yake, iPhone 11pro dinta dake kan side table ce tayi kara cikin salon kidan ringtone din Mummyn ta janyo wayar tayi tare da daidaita kanta ta daga wayar.

Mummy cikin tashin hnkl daga muryanta tace.

"Cwthart kishiya za'ayi miki amma dan ubanki baki sanar dani ba, sai yanzu nakeji a bakin excellency, wai Dad din mahir ya fada masa an Saka ranar aurensa 2months."

"humm mummy mahir ya cuceni ya zubar mini da ajina but don't worry about me."

"dan ubanki ya zakice I should not worry ke baki tashi kin ganni da kishiyaba, how will you cope, kuma the most annoying part shine kinsan mahir ba wani sonki yake sosai ba, Cwthart am worried about ur happiness."

Mummy ta karsa mgnr kmr zatayi kuka, feena da idanuwanta suka gama kadawa tayi wani shu'umin murmushi wanda duk tym din da tayishi to ba sauki dakyar tace.

"Mum karki damu feena ce fa jininki kuma haihuwarki rainonki mummy I will soon be... "

Sae tayi shiru kuma tana girgiza kai.

"what do you mean?"

Mummy ta tmby ta cikin sauri, shiru tayi tana tunanin ta fadawa Mum planing dinta tunda she's best friend kuma tasan meybe ma Mum tafita wata bakar idea din.

Mummy ta katse shirun da fadin.

"Cwthart plz tell me, am worried."

"karki damu Mummyna Zan shigo villa this days."

"okay dear zan turo jet gobe ya dauko mini ke."

"tohm mummy."

"that's my baby take care. "

Daga haka suka datse wayar feena ta mike tsaye tana zagaye parlour at the same times kuma ta fashe da wata irin dariya.

"BA A WARWARE ALKAWARI DA NI HIBBATULLAH.... Ni da ke shege ka fasa........."

_A gaggauce_

*🅿 27*

"Mum duba kiga uban kayan da yayi yar matsiyatan nan."

"I don't care, abnd ke damuna kawai shine yarda zan dubi kawayena akan za'ayiwa 'yata kishiya mace kamata."

"Mummy Dr mahir is getting married."

"mum my heartbeats for him wllh zan kashe kaina mummy."

"yah subhanallah why Dear me yayi zafi kul karna sake ji zokiji wata mgn."

Mummy ta fada tana rada mata mgn a kunne, ta6e baki feena tayi tare da mikewa tsaye tana goge hawayen fuskarta but still wasu na zubowa, cikin tsananin 6acin rai tace.

"Mum na hkr ya aurota."

****

Shirye shiryen biki ya kankama domin saura kwana biyar kacal a daura aure idan Kaga amarya hibba bazaka ganeta ba sbd tsabar kyan da tayi, gyaran jiki na musamman na kasaitattun mata unty shuwa ke mata unty ramla ce ta samu handle nata a IG haj shuwa001 kwararriyar mai gyaran jiki ce idan tayi gyaran jiki umm ba'a mgn, IN tsaya fada muku yarda hibba ta koma 6ata lkc NE just imagining classic bride.

Haka ma wajen mahir sosai yake shirye shiryensa da abokanensa musamman anwar da yake jansa sosai a kowacce harka sae dea shi anwar din yana karfin haline kawai amma da akaganshi kasan yana cikin damuwa mai tarin yawa.

Hibba ce ta fito daga dakinta da alama daga barci ta tashi hayaniyar unty ramla da unty zee ce ta tasheta, a zazzaune ta gansu a parlo sun baje siyayyar kayan kitchen da suka karasa siyowa a gaban umma, unty zee ta shigo da flate din dan wanke da ta Zubo masu.

"kintashi kenan?"

"inafa hayaniyar wadannan ce ta tashen. "

unty zee tace.

"tunda bataso abarshi kawai ba sae anyi ba."

sake gyara zama tayi tana fuskantanshi tare da tagumi tana wani irin murmushi shi kuwa inbanda kallonta babu abinda yake yabi fatar jikinta da kallo yaga tayi wani sumul da ita.

Girgiza kai tayi sannan ta daga wayar tana kallonshi kmr yarda yake kallonta.

"unty fareeda ina wuni."

"lfy sister in-law, wadda zatayi arrange pictures tare da makeup artist zasuzo tare da driver around 4 o'clock zasu daukeki zuwa government house anan za'ayi muku pictures din."

Kallon mahir tayi tana masa alamar me zatace ya daga mata hannu tare da ta6e baki alamar be sani ba, cike da inda inda tace.

"okay tohm."

Wayar mahir ya kar6a tare da saka speaker yace. 

"wai meyasa kika cika takurane shi pre-wedding din dole ne yinsa ne."

"oh mara mutunci Ashe biyoya kayi ka zugeta to dolene sae anyi dan baka isa kasa nayi asaran kudina a bnz ba."

"ki fada a biyaki mna."

"dallah chan stubborn kawai, baka da kudin da zaka biyani."

Dariya yayi sannan yace.

"mum dinta tace baza'ayi ba ta kasa fada miki ne."

"karya kake ae munyi waya da umman ynz kuma ta amince ni dallah bani ita muna mgn."

Hibba ta dauki wayar tare da cirewa a speaker suka cigaba da mgn kafin ta kashe.

hira suka danyi kafin yayi mata sallama ya tafi. 

a sanyaye tashigo gidan, umma ta fito daga bayi ta kalleta sannan tace.

"nadauka zeshigo yaci dan wanke."

"yace bayajin yunwa."

Ta fada sannan ta kuma cewa.

"yauwa Umma wae wae..."

Umma ta katseta da fadin.

"takirani na kuma amince ni dai fatana ki kiyaye."

Cike da farinciki hibba tayiwa Umma godia tashige wajen su unty ramla.


Around 4 kuwa hibba na idar da sallahr LA'asar motar daukanta tazo, ta fita sanye da hibba dinta tsayawa fadar kyan motar Tab cin read more guda ne🤣 Back sit aka bude mata tashiga nan idanunta ya fada cikin nasa dake kishingide yana duba jarida, kallo daya tayi masa ta dauke kanta shima bece mata komai ba har suka isa government house hibba tasha zallar mamaki wae yau itace a gdn gwamnati hka tashigeshi kmr yarda take shiga gidansu Tab.

Wani katon building suka shiga a wani hadedden parlour nan take aka fara tsara mata makeup simple mai kyau sai nan take ta fara wani kyallin hasken kyau wata tsadaddiyar lafara aka nada mata tayi kyau mahir shima ya gama shirinsa ya fito nan aka fara kashesu da hotuna masu azabar kyau wajen color goma sukayi duk da kaya iri iri a guri daban daban kuma duk cikin hoton babu irin na fitsarar nan sunyine cikin wayewa da ilimin Islam. 

Sae bayan magriba suka koma gida.






the following days.

Ranar lunch eve. 

A marhaba akayi katon holl din nan yasha uban decorations mai shegen kyau da tsada, holl ya cika makil da yan uwa da kawaye kowa ya kece raini iya raini yan ni da Dr mahir fans har dan ankonsu ansha kyau nan na juya ko zanga yan amcy novels amma ko mutun daya ban gani sae lkcn na Tuna da Ashe team uwargidane🤣


Daga gidan makeup mahir tare da abokinsa Yarima Asim (bakar kaddara), sae a gaba Rashida da itama taci gayu iya gayu ko za'a dace🤣

idan kukaga kyan da two couples din nan sukayi sae kun dimoce domin sunyi masifar azavar kyau cikin shigarsu. 

Tunda suka shiga motar babu me mgn cikinsu can dae ya riko hannunta a hnkl yana kallon hadadden zanen lallainta, a hnkl yake messaging hannun murya kasa kasa yace. 

"love why are you not looking at me? "

"nothing."

"okay look at me. "

Ya fada idanunsa kanta

"wow looking so cute."

Ya fada yana kissing handa dinta.

*🅿 28*

Ayi lunching lfy washe gari ya kama dinner wadda akayita a favs itama sosai tayi kyau ta tsaru fiye da lunching domin an Tara big guys hadaddu natashin hnkl hatta da anwar yazo dinner din bisa ta kurawar mahir da yayi masa.

Karfe 12:30 aka tashi daga wajen dinner a hanyar anwar koma gida aka kirashi immediately meeting Abuja wanda za'a zauna 3 na dare a fadar shugaban kasa tare da manyan doctors akan yajin aikin da akeyi a manyan asibitocin Nigeria.

Relaxing yayi bayan mota ransa na 6aci domin babu yarda za'ayi ya tafi Abuja ana gobe za'a daura masa aure, suna isa Gida ko kyakkyawan parking driver beyiba ya fito tare da shiga cikin gidan, beji motsin feena ba har ya isa apartment dinta, bata parlour ya shige bedroom dinta kwance yagnta kasan tiles da alama barci ne ya dauketa, gabanta ya matsa tare da tsugunnawa dai dai kanta a hankula ya dagata kmr Baby ya kwantar da ita saman bed a hnkl ta bude manyan idanunta da suka kankance tsabar kuka ta sauke akansa, tsurawa juna ido sukayi sai hawaye suka fara zubo mata, a nutse ya dago da ita tare da shigar da ita jikinsa ya rungumeta tsam, kuka mai karfi ta sakar masa tana sake tusa kanta sosai cikin kirjinsa. 

Sai da tayi kukan me isarta shide idonsa lumshe yanajin kukan nata har cikin ransa.

Dago kanta tayi ta kalleshi cike da ban tausayi tace.

"me yasa ka tsaneni mahir menayi maka kayi mini irin wannan mummunar tsanar da har zakayi aure ka gagara sanar dani meye matsayina a wajenka?"

Ta karasa maganar wasu zafaffun hawaye na zubo mata.

Ta katseshi da sauri.

Wayanshi ta sake ringtone yana dubawa yaga kirane, dubarta yayi sannan yace.

"dear kirane daga Mr President zamuyi meeting game da yajin aikin da ake, but...."

*🅿 29*

Cikin wannan dare mahir ya wuce Abuja daga shi sae drivern sa, feena kuwa cikin dare zazza6i mai zafi ya rufeta. 


A hanya mahir kishingide bayan mota yana karanta Alkur'ani mai girma cikin app na wayarsa, haka kawai yaji gabansa na faduwa jikinsa yayi wani irin sanyi, karamar wayansa ya dauko tare da danna number hibba,kamar jira take tayi saurin dagawa cikin sanyin murya tace.

"yah hanya?"

Lumshe idanu yayi yanajin wani karyewar zuciya dabe ta6ajin irinsa ba murya kasa kasa yace. 

"yanzu haka muna kd mekikeyi har 3:15 baki bacci ba?"

"hub nakasa gabana na faduwa haka kawai..."

Sae kuma ta fashe masa da kuka, sake lumshe milk eyes nashi yayi sannan ya bude shima har cikin ransa yakejin sonyin kukan amma ya daure yace. 

"haba love mene na kuka kuma? Kamar ba musulma ba kin manta da addu'ar da annabin mu ya bamu idan munajin irin wannan yanayin? Love plz stop cry just kitashi kiyi alwala kiyi sallah meybe faduwar gaban na alaka da auren mu da ake shirin daurawa gobe."

Shiru tayi tana cigaba da shesshekan kukanta, cikin sanyin murya tare da tarin laushi yace. 

"baby I love you with all my I don't miss u in my life I love you baby forever and ever."

Har tsakiyan kanta kalamansa Suka ratsata ta kuwa sake fashe da wani sabon kukan dake dukan zuciyar mahir, dakyar ya iya sake furta.

"love mene?"

"my heart is broken."

"sorry swthart duk abinda nace kina fada kinji."

A sanyaye ta amsa masa, nan ya fara karanto addu'o'i tana amsa masa, wani irin wawan burki driver ya saki yayin da motar ta kwace masa sukayi daga daga suka fada wani katon rami katuwar motar da ta kwara musu itama tayi kansa, wani irin ihu driver ya saki yayin da mahir idanuwansa ya lumshe cikin tsananin rudu da tashin hankali yake furta innalillahi WA'inna ilaihir'raji'un la ilaha illahu muhammadur'rasulullah....... "


Wata muguwar kara hibba ta Saki wadda taja hankalin duk mutanen gidan masu barci da wadanda ke tsakar gida suna tuwon gobe, umma tayi saurin shigowa dakin da hibba suke ciki, ganin tsananin rudu da tashin hankalin dake fuskar hibba yasa umma yin saurin zuwa gabanta tare da rikota, sae dae ashe tuni hibba ta suma umma na kamota ta fadi kasa rigijif dukkan wasu sassa na jikinta na sandarewa😓


Hakakika masoya wannan labarin ina me baku hakuri bisa babban rashin jigo da mukayi wato mahir sae dea ku sani kullu nafsin za'iqatul maut tabbas duk wani mai rai a doron duniyar nan sai ya dandani dacinta da zafinta, kuma kowa zaman jiranta yake, bazan Iya tsayawa fada muku irin halin da yan uwa da abokananen arziki kai da duk wani wanda yasa doctor mahir ya shigaba bisa rasuwarsa, anan aka kwashi gawawwakinsu zuwa yola, dai dai lokacin daurin auren sa akayi masa jana'iza tare da miliyoyin mutane suka rakashi gidansa na gaskiya😭

Hakika bamu da tsumi bare dabara yh rabb munyi tawassali da kyawawan sunayenka yh Allah kasa mutuwa ta zama Hutu a rayuwarmu ubangiji dukkan al'uman musulmin da suka rigamu gidan gaskiya Allah ya gafarta masu yh rabb ka haskaka kabarin duk wani musulmi da ke da digon imani a zuciyarsa Allah muntuba Allah ka yafe mana😭


Kowa ka zaga zakaga yarda yake kuka yana fadin alkhairan mahir gareshi.

A hankali ta fara bude idanuwanta dasukayi mata azabar nauyi tun tana ganin dishi dishi har idanun suka bude gaba daya ta fara bin dakin asibitin da kallo umma tare da wata yayar babane zaune gefanta daka gansu kasan sunci kuka kuma suna kan cinsa, hibba cikin nauyi baki ta fara kokarin mgn sae dea ina ta kasa wata muguwar kara ta Saki tare da fara fizge fizge, umma tayi kanta tana rirriketa ita kuma umma sarai tayi waje da gudu tana kuka tana kiran doctor, wasu alluran masu zafi na bacci suka sake mata.



6angaren feena tab nawawo kawai kuyi imagining mahaukaciya number daya domin ita daddauretama akayi domin allurar baccinma taki daukanta. 



Bayan kwana bakwai.

Yau akayi sadakar bakwai ta rasuwar mahir wadda zan Iya cewa kusan duk wani kusa na Africa dama Nigeria sun halacci sadakar nan, a ranar kuma mutuwar ta dawo sabuwa. 

Hibba tana zaune a wani daki dake gidan su mahir kana ganinta zaka zubar mata da kwalla domin duk ta jeme cikin kwanakin nan ta rame domin ko ruwa bata Iya sakawa bakinta inba na alwala ba.

Unty farida ce tashigo rike da plate da gorar faro ta ajiye gaban hibbar.

Itama duk ta rame tayi wani iri ga idanuwanta da suka wani kukkumbura cikin sanyi murya tace.

"hibba ga abinci ki daure kici inji mum."

Hibba ta dago ta kalleta idanuwanta na kawo ruwa zatayi mgn unty farida ta saki wani irin kuka tare da rungume hibbar tana wani irin kuka.

Unty zarah da ta fito daga toilet din dakin ta share hawayenta da suka zubo mata ta fice a dakin tsabar tausayin Halin da dangin mahir suka shiga na rashinsa dama ita kanta hibbar.


A ranar su hibba sukayi shirin komawa kano duk da Mum taso abar hibba amma yanayin da ita kanta mum din ke ciki yasa Abban mahir din yace kawai su koma da hibba dan barinta wajen Mum din sake lalata komai ne domin kullum zasu dawwama cikin kuka ne, feena kawai aka bari tare da masu kula da ita har ta gama takaba. 




Kwanci tashi babu wuya wajen Allah yau har anyi wata guda da rasuwar mahir.

Hibba na tsakar gida sanye da T-shirt da zanin atamfa tana wanke wanke, kallo daya zaka mata ka gano tsananin damuwa dake tare da ita duk ta LAlace ta jame.

Sallamar umma ce tasa dago da kanta a hankali ta amsa mata cikin sanyi da rashin son magana. 

Umma ta cire hijjab dinta tare da shanyawa kan igiya cike da tausayawa tace.

"sannu hibbatullah ke da nace ki hakura da wanke wanken nan idan na dawo nayi ke da bakyajin dadi."

Hibba tayi shiru kafin can tace.

"ae basu da yawama na kusa karasawa."

Umma ta jijjiga kai harcin ranta takejin rashin dadin wannan sanyin da rashin mgnr da hibba takeyi ynz.

"shikkenan Allah yayi miki albarka."

A ciki ta amsa da amin din, dai dai shigowar anwar tare da sallama, umma ce kawai ta amsa a fili banda hibba da ta sake kasa da kanta tana cigaba da abinda take. 

Har kasa ya tsugunna yana gaida umma, umma ta amsa cikin sakin fuska tare da tambayar jikinsa da granny domin shima yasha fama da rashin lfy bayan rasuwar mahir din.

Umma ta miko masa kujera ita kuma ta shige dakin, a jima yana noticing yanayin hibba gaba daya ta chanja ta zama so silent domin hatta da doguwar mgn ta dena, numfashi ya sauke tare da furta.

"hibba me yasa kike son sakawa kanki damuwa akan abinda gba dayan mu muma ita mukejira? Wannan abinda kikeyi sam ba shine cikakken masoyi ya kamata yayiwa masoyinsa ba, abu daya ynz mahir ke bukata har gaban abada a gare shine addu'a matukar zaki dinga takura rayuwarki kina sawa kanki damu da iyayenki da yan uwanki to mahir baze ta6a alfahari da ke ba, hibba wanda ya rasu ya rasu baze dawoba kinsan da wannan mahir ya rigamu zuwa gidan gaskiya kuma alhmdllh sae dea muyi masa murna domin  muna kyautata masa zaton dacewa da rahmar ubangiji domin annabi yace duk wanda ya cika da kalimatush shahada to tabbas ze samu cetona! To hibba anan wata makoma kike tunanin mahir ze tafi tabbas duk wata alama ta rahamar ubangiji tna tare da mahir ko dan kyakkyawar shedar mutane da yake samu tare kyakkyawan karshe da yasamu domin ajalinsa yazone a hanyarsa ta zuwa ceto dumbin rayukan talakawa da ake rasawa saka makon yajin aikin da asibitoci keyi, toh ynz dan Allah hibba ko wannan bazesa muyiwa mahir kyakkyawan zato ba? Mu tayashi murna da farincikin dacewa ba sannan muyiwa junanmu muma addu'ar samu babban rabo irin nasa ba?"

shiru yayi yana kallo yarda ta kife kanta tsakanin cinyoyinta tana kuka kamar ranta ze fita, tabbas duk cikin kalaman anwar babu karya a ciki domin itace babbar sheda kancewa mahir ya rasu da ambaton Allah a bakinsa, tabbas babu amfanin wannan damuwa ta ta sai dea har gaban abada bazata Iya kankare digon soyayyar mahir daga cikin zuciyarta da magudanar jini da numfashinta ba, gsky tayi babban rashin da bazata Iya samun madadinsa ba, domin mahir haskene a rayuwarta kuma babban bangon dafawa a rayuwarta. 

Sae da tayi kukanta me isarta yayin da anwar begaza wajen kwantar mata da hankali ba ta hanyar nasiharsa da lallashinta. 


har sae da yaga ta dan Saki kadan, yana dan janta da hira ta amsa mishi sama sama har ya tashi ze tafi sukayi sallama da umma ita kuma ta rakoshi soro ya tsaya tare da dafa kofa yana kallonta, cikin sanyi yace. 

"plz ki cire duk wani damuwa mahir addu'a ke so a gurinmu kawai Allah ya gafarta masa yasa idan tamu tazo mu cika da imani."

"amin."

Ta fada kanta a kasa.

Ya danyi murmushi dake fito da zallar kyawunsa yace. 

"gobe zaki koma gurin aiki ko?"

Ta dago dasauri ta kalleshi tare dayin narai narai da ido zatayi kuka tace.

"na hkr da aikin nabari bazan Iya ba."

"karkice haka hibba aikin kine kawai ze rage miki damuwar da kike ciki kuma kinga...."

"bazan cigaba da zuwa inda zan dinga saka ran ganin hub ba."

Daga haka tabar wajen tare da komawa ciki abinta. 




*YOLA*

Mum ta zauna gefan bed din feena dake kwance amma ba barci take ba.

A hnkl Mum tace.

"feena ki tashi kici abinci kiyi sallah ki samu kisha magani ai bazaki ta zama da ciwo a jiki ba, mahir ya rasu kuma kinsan ba dawowa zeyiba har abada tabbas ansan mutuwar miji da daci amma wannan abinda kike babu inda ze kaiki ace kiki ci kiki sha sallahr ma sae anyi ta fama kiyita ga ciwo kinki shan magani ya kike abu kamar wadda baki da ilimin Islam."

Feena ta Mike zaune idanuwane kawai a fuskarta tsabar rama ga wani baki da tayi ashe daman ana hadawa da bliching, ta kalli Mum tare da turo baki tace.

"shi isa nace ku mayar dani wajen uwa da ubana tunda ku ba kaunata kuke ba ya za'ayi mijina ya mutu kuma kiyi tunanin sake ganin walwalata ko dan ke....."

Wata tsawa najwa ta daka mata dai dai shigowarta dakin tare da unty fareeda. 

Najwa tace.

"ashe har ynz baki da mutunci to kiji da kyau daman wanda ya hada ya raba albarkacin takabar dan uwanmu kikeci kawai dan da tuni mun manta dake wace a wajenmu karki kuskura ki zagar mana uwa diyar mararsa tarbiya."

Ran feena yayi mugun 6aci ta Mike zaune tare da dura wata uwar ashar kai bakace mai takaba bace suka fara musayar baki da najwa da fareeda Mum tayi iyakar ta su bari suka ki kuka Mum ta fashe da shi Tana fadin.

"yau wata dana guda a kabarinsa amma gashi kun zauna kuna debe albarka akan shi duk cikinku babu wanda na isa nace yabari yaji maganata yabari shikkenan kuyi duniya taji ta zagemu burinku ya cika ai."

Ta karasa mgnr cikin kuka tana barin dakin, su najwa sukabi bayanta suna lallashi feena kuwa tsaki taja tare da janyo wayarta da ta 6oye karkashin pillow jin motsin Mum din dazu ta cigaba da chatting dinta.🤔feena ba ilimin Islam fa.






Haka dai aka cigaba da fadi tashi a lamarin duniya, yayin da ynz kusan kullum anwar yana tare da hibba yana sake kwantar mata da hankali hakama yan uwnta dana mahir duk babu wanda aka bari a baya wajen jan hibba a jiki duk dan ta cire damuwar da take ciki.

Rayuwa haka ta cigaba da ja da dadi ba dadi, har feena ta fita daga takaba,  ta koma gidansu tare da fitar mata tuminin takaba daga gadon mahir.



Yau hibba tana dakinta barci rana ya dauketa bayan ta gama shan kukanta da ta saba, cikin barci tayi mafarkin mahir sanye da wasu kaya na alhariri kansa dauke da wata hula ta tattacen gold yana cikin wani irin katon pool mai girmn gaske ga wasu kyawawan furanne a cikin ruwa.

Daga can nesa ta farajin karar wata sarkar kafa ta zinare nan taga ta iso itama cikin wani ado a nutsu yna fitar mata da kyakkyawan murmushinsa ya fita daga ruwan ya isa gabanta, kneeling down yayi gabanta yana murmushi wanda itama shi take masa, kalmar karshe da ya fada mta lkcn mutuwarsa ya sake maimaita mata sae kuma ya saketa tare da mikewa ya koma cikin ruwan yana tafiya yana waiwayenta yana daga mata hannu yana murmushi har ya kure mata.

Zumbur ta Mike gumi jargab ya jikata dakin nata ta farabi da kallo sae taga kmr zata ganshi mikewa tayi tafara neme neme.........

*🅿 34*
