*🅿 39*

Sosae hibba take laulayin cikinta domin ko ruwa tasha sae ta amayar, har cikin ya cika wata shidda, lokacin cikin ya fito sosae ga shi cikin kwata kwata ba sata kyauba tayi dan duhu duk da cewa kowa ya kalli fatarta yasan tanajin dadi amma hakan ba hanata dan duhu ba, hancinta ya zama kato ga wani kumburi da tayi sosae, kusan kullum ta kalli kanta a mirror sae tayi kukan ganin yarda ta koma haka mahir zae sata gaba yana bata hakuri tare da sake tabbatar mata da irin so da kaunar da yake mata.

Suhaila kuwa rai fas dan ita cikin ya amsheta sosae a ynz cikin watansa hudu ya dan fito kadan domin ita jikinta yabi ga wani haske da ta sakeyi sosai.

Kullum mum cikin mitar mahir ya kawo hibba take yau suka shirya zuwa yolar, karfe biyu na rana suka sauka, Abba lokacin yazo fita kenan yana zaune a bayan mota su hibba suka karasa suka gaisa Abba sosae ya tausayawa hibbar ganin halin da take ciki shi kuwa mahir wani kyau na musamman ya yaqara yayi wata irin kiba ga haskensa da ynz har kashe ido yake.

6angaren Mum sukaje, Mum na ganinsu ta taso cike da farinciki, hibba tayi saurin tsugunnawa kamar yadda ta sabawa mum din cike da girmamawa zata gaidata mum ta rikota da sauri tare da dagota cikin fada tace.

"karki sake wannan kneeling din bakya ganin halin da kike ciki karki sake banso."

Cikin girmamawa ta gaida mum din itama ta amsa fuskarta na fitar da tsananin farincikin da take ciki, masu aiki ne suka fara shigo da tsarabar da sukayowa mum din, mum ta Kalli mahir ta dan masa hararar wasa tace.

"ka kyauta mutumin kurum."

Mahir da yasan laifinsa a wajen mum din yayi saurin cewa.

"mum nifaba bani da lafiya bazan Iya doguwar..."

"karya kake bakayi kama da marar Lfy ba."

Mum ta katseshi da sauri tana kara daka masa harara. 

Fuska ya dan marairaice ya kuma cewa.

"wolah mum bani da lafiya tmby luv ma kiji mum dan Allah nima ae bani da lfy ko?"

Ya karasa mgnr yana kannewa hibbar ido daya, tayi saurin dukar da kanta kasa ganin a gaban mum ne, ita kuwa Mum dariya ce ta kwace mata tare da daukar pillow ta cilla masa tana dariya tace.

"get out from my room."

Yana dariya ya fice.

Mum tace.

"mutumin kurum."

Hannun hibbar Mum ta kamo suka shige cikin bedroom din Mum ciki aka kawowa hibbar abinci, da taga masa ta zage taci sosae da miyar alayyahu, shima Mum mai aiki tasa takai masa nashi part dinsa, hibba na zaune kan sofar dakin Mum, mum ta gyara zama ta Kalli hibbar tace.

"yah jikin naki da fatan son yana kula dake yarda ya kamata?"

Hibba ta gyada kai alama eh. 

Mum tace.

"hibba karki 6oyen komai ki daukeni a matsayin ummanki, remember amanarki aka bani ke kamar farida da najwa kike a wajena, karkiji kunyar fada mini abinda mahir yake miki wanda bakyaso ki fada mini dan banso ya zamana yana cutar da ke."

Hibba sosae taji dadin abnd mum ta fada wata sabuwar kirmar matar ta sake gani a idanuwanta, tace.

"Mum wllh babu abinda yake mini muna zaune lafiya."

Ta fada kanta kasa.

Mum cikin jindadi tace.

"good, haka nakeso daman, ynz inane yake miki ciwo?"

"babu ko ina mum just kasala nd ciwon kafa."

"wannan is normal soon komai ze wuce dear zaki zauna a nan gurina har ki haihu? "

Dasauri hibba tace.

"eh mum zanzauna."

Tafada tana danne dariyar ta domin tasan mahir zae ga uku idan ba ita yarda ya saba da muguwar jarabarsa. 

Mum tace.

"shikkenan, ynz yi kwanciyarki ki huta."

Hibba tayi kokarin mikewa, Mum ta riko hannunta tare da nuna mata bed dinta tace.

"ki kwanta anan."

ba musu hibba ta haye gadon tare da lumshe idanuwanta tanajin kaunar mum din sosai cikin ranta, mum na fita parlonta taga mahir kwance kan kujera yana waya, mum ta doki kafarsa ya Mike zaune ta zauna kusa da shi a takaice tace.

"hibba zata zauna a nan har Allah ya sauketa lafiya."

Kutmar wolah baze yuwuba ya fada cikin ransa, a zahiri kuwa kusan kwarewa yayi da Lemon da yake sha ya ajiye gefen side table yace. 

"mum idan ta zauna nan waze dinga dafa mini abinci? "

Harara Mum ta daka mishi tare da ware masa hannu tace.

"gidanku nace kaji mini dan banza da wa yake dafama abincin? Jinake da masu aiki ko restaurant ne suke hidima dake OK ynz ma na gane dan rashin imani Kana ganin yarinyar zama dakyar tashi dakyar amma dan ubanka Kake barinta tana girki."

"no mum to wai ma ita da bakinta tace zata zauna?"

Yafadan sounding out of word. 

"nice nace zata zauna da magana ne?"

Idanu ya zaro domin sarai yasan halin mum din yace. 

"kuma tace zata zauna?"

"kai banson iskanci tashi Ka fice mini daga parlour ."

Mum ta fada tana nuna mishi hanyar fita da sauri ya Mike yayi hanyar bedroom yanajin tana cewa karya shiga yatadata daga barci amma yayi banza da ita ya shige, girgiza kai mum tayi tanajin dadin saurin da ta gani a tare da dan nata.

A tsakiyar bed din ya ganta tayi daidai tana barcinta, hayewa kan gadon yayi ko takalmin kafarshi be cireba, hibba ta bude idanuwanta tana kallonshi yace. 

"haba luv yazaki amincewa Cwthart zaki zauna anan bayan kinsan lalurata? "

Marairaice fuska hibba tayi tare da fadin.

"ya zanyi hub I can't say no wolah."

"you can't say no koh? To bari kiji yarda kika amince yasin haka zaki koma kice baki amince ba, inba haka ba yasin duk weekend ina nan kuma kinfi kowa sanin sauran."

Dariya hibba ta fashe da ita, cikin tsokana tace.

"gaskiya ni bazan Iya zuwa na fada mata haka ba, amma kai kaje Ka fada mata."

Ta fada tana mikewa zaune tana lashe lips dinta da harshenta. 

"daman nasa wllh kekikace zaki zauna da ita, amma karki damu nasan maganinki."

Yafada yana kama bakinta, zare bakinta tayi da sauri tana fadin.

"meye maganin nawa?"

Takalmin kafarsa ya zare tare da hayewa kan gadon yana janyota jikinsa, ta fara mutsumutsu tana fadin.

"hub meye hakan wae?"

"kije kicewa Mum zaki bini gida just."

Ta fada yana zura hannunsa cikin rigarta, tureshi tayi tare da fadin.

"tashi muje karani toh."

Bamusu ya Mike ya dira daga gadon, ta mike sae da sukazo bakin door ta turashi da karfi tare da rufe kofar da sauri tana dariya, tanajin yarda yaja kwafa yabar wajen, ko da ya fito Mum bata parlon a main parlour ya ganta zaune da masu aikinta suna hira baki ya turo kmr yaro yana harbin iska Mum tabi bayansa tana dariya.



Washe gari.

Mahir yazo komawa kano kiri Kiri hibba taki binsa, ya kalli Abba fuska a marairaice yace.

"abba kana gani ko? "

Abba yayi murmushi yace. 

"hajiya Asma'u kibarshi ya tafi da matarsa."

Mum ta kalli hibba da tayi kasa da kanta saboda kunyar abba tace.

"kibi mijinki hibba idan kinaso."

Hibba ta girgixa kai tace.

"mum zan zauna da ke."

Mahir ya kalleta da sauri kmr ze hadiye zuciya tsabar takaici mum tayi murmushi tacewa Abba.

"kadaeji abinda tafada da bakinta wahala yake bata girki yake sata."

Mum ta fada cikin dariya.

Mahir yayi saurin cewa. 

"Mum wllh karya nake gata ki tAmbayeta wllh bata girki da kanta."

Abba ya dafashi yace. 

"sorry son kabar Mum kasan halinta, kamar yau ne matarka zata dawo kaje Allah ya kiyaye hanya."

Kamar zeyi kuka ya mike mum tana dariya sosae tace.

"Allah ya tsare hanya son."

ko juyowa beyi ba Abba ya nunata da dan yatsa shima yana dariya kasa kasa,abba ne yacewa hibba taje ta rakashi mota, ba musu ta Mike tabi bayan mahir din.

Har ya shiga bayan mota ko kallonta beyiba sae da ya zauna sannan ya kalleta a hankali yayi mata alama tazo, ba musu ta zagaya ta shiga motar hannunta ya riko kmr zeyi kuka yace. 

"yanzu baby ni zakiwa haka koh? "

Marairaice fuska tayi tace.

"ni kunya nakeji ban Iya cewa..."

Harararta yayi yana fadin.

"karki damu yan mata idan ma gudu kikeyi zaki dinga ganina duk weekend kina bani hakkina."

Dariya tayi tare da jan dogon hancinsa ta kai bakinta zatayi kissing dinsa ya kauda fuskanshi gefe yana fadin.

"malan hudu idan ja mota mu tafi."

Jin abinda ya fada yasa ta fita daga motar da sauri tana dariya, shi kuwa wani bakin spec ya dauko ya manna a fuskarsa driver yaja motar suka bar harabar gidan. 



The following days 

Mahir yana zaune a office dinsa, wayanshi tayi kara ko da ya duba dad din feena ke kiransa sae lkcn ma ya tuna da feena tun bayan yanke mata hukunci cikin girmamawa ya daga wayar tare da sallama. 

Kuka yaji mummy ta fashe masa da shi tana fadin.

"yanzu abinda kayi mahir ka kyauta karfa ka manta duk abinda Nafisa takeyi tanayi saboda kai amma sbd kaddara ta fada mata shine ka wofantar da ita ka manta da ita sbd tsantsar butulci ko ka fada mini ka sakar mini 'ya ko kuwa?"

Ta fada cikin fada kuma still kuka take. 

Mahir yayi shiru domin basan me zece ba, ran mummy ya sake 6aci a fusace ta sake cewa.

"wllh tir da halin feena koma meye ita ta janyowa kanta da ta za6i jefa kanta halaka akan namiji shikkenan kasani wllh Allah sae ya sakawa mata mugu azzalumi."

daga haka ta datse wayar yanajin yarda take kuka sosae kmr ranta ze fita, tab gsky tsakani uwa da yaranya sae Allah tabbas mummy na tsananin kaunar feena mace mai izza ta takama hadi da jin kan tsiya itace take kuka yau kamar haka tab Allah sarki ya fada a hnkl, tabbas yana son feena a da amma rashin kula da ja ajiki yasa kwata kwata bae shaku da ita sosai ba, mugayen abubuwan da ta dinga kullawa da rashin ganin darajar sa dana iyayensa dama na aureta shine babban abnd yasa yaji baya jinta a ransa a ynz, dole ze rubuta mata takaddar saki domin baze Iya zama da ita ba.......



Yanzu hibba zuba idanu take taga ta yarda labour zezo mata sbd tana watan haihuwanta ya shiga idan kaga hibba sae ka kusan tuntsura sbd da dariya amcy kinga dae kyau irin na ruky apple ko🤣🤣sannan kinga yarda ta koma da cikin suhail tor wolah tayi kyau akan yarda hibba ta koma🤣🤣🤣lol duk wanda beson ruky ba PC me.

wasu irin pimples ne suka fito mata ga hancinta da yayi fakadede idan suna video call da fam dinta hka zasuyi ta mata dariya, umma ce kawai me fadar kyanta sae kuma mum dan hatta da mahir din shima tsiya yake mata.

Daman duk weekend din duniya yana hanyar yola mum da Abba har mamakinsa suke domin da sukayin wata biyu daya basu gashinba amma ynz kuwa kullum yana tafe. Haka ze saka hibba agaba yayi ta tuntsura mata dariya at the same times kuma ze tsugunna ne har kasa yace gwaggo ina wuni sannu haka ze sata agaba wataran sae yaga tana shirin kuka sanne ze kyaleta ya shiga kuma rarrashinta. 

Wata kwararriyar likita Mahir ya daukowa hibba daga Abuja irin baturiyace amma a Nigeria take zaune tasan aikinta sosae tana bawa hibba kula sosae domin har motsa jiki na masu haihuwa take saka ta, a ynz hibba a fusace take kullum domin cikin kwanakin nan kullum karajin nauyin cikin take ji take kmr ta yadashi ta huta, tun ranar Friday mahir yazo gashi yau har Monday be tafiba da mum tace meyasa bae tafiba sae cewa yayi baze tafiba har sae hibba ta haihu mum tace masa itafa haihuwa ba'a jiranta amma yayi fur yaki tafiya mum ta cika sosae da mamakin rashin kunyar mahir amma da ta tuna ynz abokanayensa da suka tashi tare ko wanne yana nan da yaransa sae ta share shi kawai.



Thursday 

Hibba tana zaune kasan carpet bayan ta iddar da sallahr magriba, ta Mike kafafuwanta da suka dan kumbura kadan, mahir ya bude kofa ya shigo kallon sa tayi tare da dauke kanta cos yarda takejin haushin sa Allah ne kade ya sani, dan idan taga yarda yake sake kara kyau yana wani daukar gayu ita kuma ta kallin condition din da yasata sae ta kumajin wani kululun bakinciki, murmushi ya danyi tare da zama a kusa da ita yace. 

"luv yakike? "

"lafiya."

Ta fada a takaice tana hararar sa ta gefan idanunta, wani kasaitaccen murmushi ya sakeyi wanda har sae da kyakkyawan dimples dinsa ya bayyana yace. 

"habebbty nayi wani laifi ne?"

Ba tare da ta kalleshi ba tace.

"wane yace kayi wani laifi?"

"naga tunda nazo kikejin haushina gashi yau kusan Satina guda kin hanani hakkina ma."

wata irin harara ta daka masa kmr idanuwanta zasu fado tace.

"aikin kenan kullum baka da magana sae ta hakkinka, ni baka ganin halin da nake ciki tsakanin rayuwa ko mutuwa duba kaga yarda na koma kmr wata uwar mata tashi dakyar zama dakyar ni gaskiya am tired nagaji da wannan cikin."

Ta fada tana fashewa da kuka, hannunta ya kama tare da manna masu kiss cikin tausayawa yace. 

"sannu dear sorry luv kin kusa hutawa insha Allahu ko wacce uwa haka ta sha fama, km kinsan aski idan yazo gaban goshi yafi zafi kiyi hkr kinji sannu."

Wata hararar ta sake 6alla mashi cike da tsiwa tace.

"ni banason sannun nan."

Dariya ya danyi yace. 

"toh me kikeso fada mini."

"ni banason komai kawai nagaji."

"Bari na hada miki ruwan wanka."

Ya fada yana mikewa, bayansa tabi da kallo tana sakejin wani haushin nasa tsabar masifa ta mai ciki...hka ma unty hanan take lol🤣

Fitowa yayi tare da mikar da ita tsaye sae hararsa taken shi kuma sae aekin sorry yake fadi dan yanzu yaga aboda take tak kawai take jira ta fashe, kayan jikinta ya cire mata ya fara kokarin cire nashi ta kalleshi tace.

"meye hakan? "

"wanka zamuyi."

"ah ah kaje part dinka kayi a toilet dinka."

Hannunta yaja suka shige toilet din yana gama cire kayan jikinshi, ta sake kallonsa tace.

"wae meye haka ka fita mana."

matsowa yayi daf da ita ya marairaice yace. 

"plz habebbty let's do it, naji lafiyar babyna sae kinga in Allah yasa kafin gobe kin sauka, wllh I will be lonely for a while."

Ya karasa mgnr yana kissing wuyanta, kallon katon cikinta tayi wanda ya gama yin kasa gaba daya alamar ta kusa haihu, tana mamakin halin mahir da baya gaji da abu daya, gashi itama har ya koya mata jarabarsa tureshi tayi tana turo baki ta zauna baki bowel tace.

"ni marata ciwo take mini."

"tun yaushe yake miki ciwon?"

Ya fada yana kai hannunshi kan mararta ta.

"tun dazu."

"kin fadawa Teena? Me yasa baki fada mini ba?"

"ae ba sosai yake mini ciwon ba kadan kadan."

Ta fada in I don't care tune. 

"amman luv kinsan mum tace miki duk abinda ke damunki ki dinga fada, kila you are having a contractions."

Harararshi tayi ta turo baki tare da fadin.

"idan haka ake contractions da kullum zan dinga haihu."

Janyota yayi suka shige cikin bowel na wankasae da sukayi hot romance kafin sukayi wanka suka fito yana tmbyr marar tace baya ciwo, tare sukayi sallar isha rigar bacci ya saka mata ya kwantar da ita tare da rage mata AC ya janyo mata duvet cover ya rufeta ya fita.

Yana fita ciwon Marar ya dawo, jin yarda ciwon ke dan mata yasa bata dauki abin serious ba saukowa tayi ta shiga toilet zatayi fitsari tana taugunnawa taga wani abu kmr mucus (gamsai) hade da jini, da sauri ta Mike ta mayar da pant dinta ta fito tana tada teena da ke kwance kan sofa tana bacci, ta fada mata abinda ta gani, teena tace.

"kwanta."

Cikin harshen turanci, ba musu ta kwanta, ta dauko safa ta saka ta fara dubata cikin sauri tace.

"you in the labor."

Hibba ta zaro ido cike da mamaki cikin ranta tace daman haka haihuwar take amma mata idan sukazo wajen su suke masu tumasanci nayi dariya nace hmm hibba kenan.

Guraren karfe 2 hibba ido ya fara raina fata tun tana yarfa hannu har ta fara kuka kuma har zuwa lkcn ba'a sanar dasu mahir ba, karfe 3 taji contractions ya fara zama unbelievable, ta fara sanin ba tumasanci mata keyi musu ba, ta dubi teena dake danna mata waist a wahalce tace.

"ki kirawo mini mahir wayyo mutuwa zanyi ki kirawo mum."

Ba musu teena ta danna kiran Mum ta sanar da ita then ta Kira mahir shima.

Su Mum na shigowa sukaga halin da hibba ke ciki sukayo kanta hannun Mum ta rike da karfi tana cije baki, dan mintina kadan ciwon ya tsaya ta kwanto da kanta kirjin mum bacci yana dauketa, ko minti uku batayi ba ta Fara tana Saki wani irin ihu, mum ta Kalli mahir da yayi tsaye ya rasa ma abnd zeyi kmr ba doc ba ta daka mishi tsawa tace.

"katsaya mana a kai kazo ka dubata mana."

Jikinsa na wani irin tsuma ya isa gaban hibbar yana ta6ata ta sake fasa wani kukan. 

"sorry luv sannu baby."

Ya fada wane zeyi kuka, cikin kuka da bala'i tare da radaddin nakuda🤣tace.

"naki sannu bazanyi sorryn ba ni ba kagama cutata ba kullum cikin tale mini kafa kake Kana....."

Saurin toshe mata baki yayi da hannunta gudun kartafadi wata mgnr gaban mum, Abba ne yayi knocking Mum ta fito yabata wani tofin ayatul shufa'a da yayi mata da ruwan zamzam yace abata tasha, mum ta kar6a ta kaimata dakyar hibba ta kar6a tasha iyakar galabaita ta galabaita mahir kmr ba doc ba sam ya rasa me zeyi teena ce kawai keyin aikinta har kuka sae da yayi mata ganin har asuba hibba bata haihu ba yace kawai su tafi hospital ayi mata c.s Abba yace abari dae a gani.

Haka sukayita abu daya har garin Allah ya waye sae wajen sallar juma'a hibba ta haifi santaleliyar yarinyarta Mace katuwa tubarkallah zuwa lkc hibba batasan inda kanta yake ba domin har karata sae da akayi sbd tsabar girman jaririyar, kaf gidan tun daren jia babu wanda yasha ko da ruwa sbd tsabar tashin hnkl, teena ce ta fito daga dakin cike da farinciki ta sanar da Mum ta haihu mahir na ciki yana stitching hibbar, Mum cike da farinciki ta kira Abba dake part dinsa tasanar dashi kafin tashiga dakin hibbar tanajin kukan jaririyar ko takan yarinyar batabiba tayi wajen 'yarta da take ta sauke ajiyar zuciya ba damar kuka tsabar azaba, Mum ta riko hannunta cikin tausayawa tace.

"sannu daughter Allah yayi miki albarka Allah ya albarkanci abinda kika haifa."

Idanuwan hibba a lumshe bata iya budesu Mum ta matsa gaban jariyar da teena keshiryawa ta zubawa photocopy din mahir din ido taji wani irin farinciki na mamayeta. 

mahir na gama dinkita ya riko hannunta muryarsa a dashe tsabar tausayi hibba ta bude jajayen idonta ta sauke a kansa yace. 

"am sorry habebbty for putting you through such pain, Allah bazaki kara haihuwa ba, idan muna bukatar yara we will get surrogate mother amma ke kuwa bazaki sake wannan wahalar ba."

Kukan babyn yasa ya juya tare da amsarta hannun Mum yana ganinta kmr yar wata uku a hnkl ya furta.

"masha Allah."

Mum tace.

"what did she weigh?"

Mahir yace.

"4.5kg."

Mum tace.

"that's why ta wahalar mini da daughter wato a dole sae tazo ta kamoni a kiba to kwalelenta sae dea ta tafi kano can."

"wato har an fara kishi da matar."

Abba ya fada yana shigowa dakin.

Mum ce ta hau kiran su fareeda da najwa then ta kira gidan su hibbar daga nan ta fara sanar da fam dinta hakama Abba shima fam dinsa da abokanensa ya fara sanarwa mahir ma nan ya fara buge buge yana sanar da haihuwa, ko da ya sanar da anwar sosai yayi farinciki ya koma gd yana sanar da suhaila haihuwar hibba tsabar farinciki sae ga nakuda itama tun yana dauka wasanta ne har ta fara kokarin tara masa da jama'a dama mamy tana gidan nan suka dauketa sukayi asibiti da ita.

Su unty ramla ne suka shirya tafiya can yolar Mum tace basae sunzo ba zuwa gobe hibbar zata taho gida tayi wanka a gida, kafin kace me labarin haihuwar matar Dr mahir ta fara zagaye media tare da hoton jaririyar da na mum din ta da Dad dinta da aka pic-mix dinshi sae yadawa ake a media, labarin haihuwar har villa mummy nan ta sake kuka kmr zata hadiye zuciyarta. 

suhaila mafa anci kwakwa babu karya har Allah ya sauketa lafiya ta haifi santalelen danta mai kama da ita sak 

A tare akayi sunansu yarinyar hibba taci sunan mum Asma'u suna kiranta husna dan suhaila kuma sunan Dad din anwar bashir suke kiransa walid gagarumin bikin suna akayi a fabs wanda aka kashe mahaukacin uban kudi, washe garin suna aka wuce da suhaila kd ita kuma hibba tana gdn su duk da mahir bai so haka ba.





mun kusa gamawa insha Allah.

Bayan watanni biyu. 

Abuja. 

Katafaren gida ne wanda zamu Iya kira da aljannar duniya nd kawai kuyi imagining gidan ministers a Abuja, ka tafaren parlon gidan nakeji tarin hayaniyar yaran gidan sae dariya suke suna hira har da feena na hango tare da hibba feena ynz ta mayar da jikinta wannan kyan da gwallin duk Sun dawo uwa uba hutu duk sun fara dawo mata, shigowar mahir yasa duk suka mimmike kmr yarda suka saba suka tafi zasuyi welcome hugg dinsa sae dea wannan karon hibba na tsaye gefan feena tana dariya,idanuwansa kir kan hibbar da feena can yace. 

"ban gane ba."

hibba tayi dariya tace.

"meye baka ganeba a nan matanka ko kuma yayanka."

Da ido ya nuna feenar, hibba tayi murmushi sannan itama ta ido ta nuna masa yayansa tace.

"su sukaje suka daukota, umm yau fa ango kake babu ruwanmu my kids kuzo mubar Abie da Mommah."

Ta fada tana daukar miemie, feena tace.

"ina zaku kuma?"

"rabu da ita suje din zan kamata."

Mahir ya fada yana dariyar, hibba tana dariya ta tasa yaran a gaba sukayi downstairs, yabi bayanta da kallo kmr ya hadiyeta, jiyowa yayi ya kalli feena tayi kasa zata tsugunna yayi saurin rikota tare da cewa.

"muje daga ciki zaki kar6i punishment din ki."

Ya fada yana sa6ata a kafa🤣🤣


Kwanan mahir da feena uku ana cin uwar sabada kafin ya koma dakin hibbar Sun fito daga wanka ta dauko sleeping gown dinta zata saka yana daga kwance yace. 

"Baby me zakiyi? "

"zan saka kayana mana."

"kinsan fa banson kina kwana da kaya kawai ki zo haka."

"gaskiya ni banso kasan fa..."

Sae kuma tayi shiru, hannu ya mika mata ta kama ya janyota.




Rayuwarsu gwanin ban sha'awa suna zaune lfy ko wacce na kokari wajen ganin ta kaurace abnd zesa ranta ya 6aci domin wataran mahir baya Iya control kansa a kan hibba a gaban feena wataran yake nuna son hibba, dan ma ita hibbar na kaurace faruwar hkan wata ran. 

Watan feena biyar da dawowa hibba ta fara laulayi nan hankalin hibba ya tashi sosae domin ita da kanta tasan tana shan pills na planning to ya hakan ta faru saka mahir tayi  a gaba tana kuka wiwi shidae shiru kawai yayi yana danne dariyarsa, wannan karon cikin natama mai laulayine sosae domin har a hospital ta kwanta.

Suna zaune a babban restaurant a kasar Cyprus wanda sukazo har da su anwar da iyalinsa suhaila an zama manyan mata ta Kara kyau sosai itama ynz ciki gareta laulayi take ta fama na ci.

Kowa a wajen ya mayar da hankalinsa wajen cin abinci banda hibba da tayi relax jikin kujerarta tana kallonsu domin ita duk nau'in abincin wajen babu wanda ya burgeta, mahir da tun dazu hankalinshi akan ya ke kuma kowa na wajen ya fahimci hakan ya  dauko pasta daya ta chicken shawarma ya kai bakinta,  a hnkl ta langwa6ar da kanta gefe kmr zatayi kuka tace.

"plz hub Karma ka fara I can't eat all that."

Hannunta ya riko cikin tsananin sonta yace. 

"yes amma at least you can ko da rabin of it din ne plz hayatee."

Dariya Anwar ya fashe da ita sannan yace. 

"yes Mrs minister plz try at least din, kar Man din ya fara yi mana kuka anan dan naga alamar haka."

Suhaila ta zura hannu cikin aljihun anwar tana fadin.

"baby I cant fine bani chewing gum zuciyana ya fara tashi."

Chewing gum ya dauko mata ya bata.

Feena da duk abnd suke bata dago ta kallesu ta dukufa wajen yiwa miemie wasa mahir ya kirata yace. 

"cwtappy kinga luv taki cin komai ko?"

Yarda yayi mgnr ne yasa duk suka fashe da dariya har hibbar sae da ta dara , walid yaron anwar ya zaro shawarmar gabansa ya Mike zuwa gaban hibbar yace.

"luv plz eat."

Yayi mgnr lyk mahir, dariya suka sake bushewa da ita a hnkl ta bude bakinta yasaka mata, har ta cinye, suka mike complete fam selfie stick suka dauka husna taja musu selfie.
 
 *The all life happy ever after😍😍😍*
